01-rail-density

01-rail-density: the session rail in its three densities (comfortable, compact, minimal icons), the minimal rail's tooltip, and a row marked stale

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

Comfortable shows two-line rows (name, coloured status · age); compact folds each row to one line with the age at the right, and minimal leaves a strip of tool icons with attention badges and a "!5" count. On the darker canvas the cards on Home stand off well and "Open its page" is the one solid violet button in the Needs you card; the lighter dark accent shows on it, on the logo mark, the Work Max runway bar and the rail's New session button. The error rows now read "Stopped with an error" with no cut age in comfortable-* and stale-row-1440x900. At 1024 px the top bar stays one row by showing the cleanup pill as a broom and "7" and dropping the This Mac line (comfortable-1024x768, compact-1024x768). In compact-1024x768 and compact-1440x900 the first row's name is still cut to "Fa…" and the third to "Fake D…" while the middle row shows "Fake Desk": the app tag and age squeeze the name. minimal-tooltip-1440x900 still shows no tooltip at all, so it cannot prove the tooltip it is named for. stale-row-1440x900 marks its row with a small amber "stale · 3m" chip after "Working", which reads clearly. All captures were re-rendered.

Weak

  • compact-1024x768, compact-1440x900: session names cut to "Fa…" and "Fake D…" by the app tag and age.
  • minimal-tooltip-1440x900: no tooltip is visible.
  • minimal-*: the minimal rail's icons are told apart only by tiny coloured corner dots and badges.

01-rail-sort

01-rail-sort: the rail's sort and grouping options (needs me, by project, quota risk), a search with no match, a snoozed row and the rail's View menu

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

empty-search-1440x900 handles a search with no match well: a focused field with a violet ring, "No sessions match" and a "Clear filters" button. view-1440x900 opens the View menu with icons and short hints ("Inactive · in an app's list, not running"), a check mark on Ended today and "Only Account/Project · All ›"; it covers the page heading and the rail's Account tab while open, which is expected of a popover. The lighter dark accent shows on the logo mark, the search ring, "Open its page" and the Work Max runway bar, and the top bar stays one row. The rail's error rows now read "Stopped with an error" without a cut age. The sort captures still do not show their sort: needs-me-1440x900 is pixel-for-pixel the default Home of 01-rail, group-by-project-1440x900 looks the same, and nothing on screen names the active sort or grouping. snoozed-1440x900 still shows "snoozed 12m" as a small grey chip on the boundary between the "Limit reached · 0% left" row and the "Asks you · 2 h" row, where it reads as belonging to either. quota-risk-1440x900 was not re-rendered and still shows an older layout (Home, Accounts, History, Settings, More in the header, old filter chips and sort selects), so it doesn't match the rest of the family.

Weak

  • group-by-project-1440x900, needs-me-1440x900: identical to the default Home; the active sort or grouping is not visible anywhere.
  • snoozed-1440x900: the "snoozed 12m" chip sits between two rows, not inside the row it belongs to.
  • quota-risk-1440x900: not re-rendered; shows an older header and filter layout.

01-rail

01-rail: the session rail beside Home, with every status, with no accounts (welcome) and with one account still to sign in, at four widths in both themes

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

The Needs you, Runway by account and Pick up where you left off cards sit a clear step above the canvas in dark, and in light the white cards stand off the grey canvas; "Open its page", "Find my accounts" and "New session" are solid violet and read as the one main action in their card. In dark the lighter accent shows on the logo mark, "Open its page", the Work Max runway bar and the rail's New session button, and it reads a little brighter against the near-black. The top bar stays one row at 1024 to 1440 px ("This Mac · Plenty of room", "7 to clean up"). The rail is dense but legible: project headers, two-line rows with status in colour and a time, and the selected row marked by a bar. The status line now shows its age whole or not at all, so both error rows in all-statuses-1280x800-* and 1440x900-* read "Stopped with an error" with no cut "· 7…"; the error state is still amber in the rail like "Probably stuck", while the session header shows it in red. The second pass keeps rail times whole: determinism-a (App grouping) now reads "ai-j… · 3 h ago" beside "Probably asks you", cutting the project instead of the age. In ready-home-* at 1024 to 1440 px the first need ("Work Max needs its sign-in") now gives its detail on two lines, ending whole on "…AI Juggler never sees your password." instead of one cut line, and the card grows to fit.

At 390 px the cleanup pill is now a broom with "7", so the controls fit one row under the nav, but the header is still three rows (nav on two lines, then the controls); the Needs you rows still cut their status to a few letters ("Stopped wi…", "Limit reach…"); the first need's detail now takes two lines (all-statuses-390x844-* shows "storefront · AI Juggler · Personal" whole), though in ready-home-390x844-* it still ends cut at "…about thirty second…"; and in honest-empty-390x844-* the "We found Claude Code on this computer." chip still breaks with its icon alone on the first line. In ready-home-* the runway still lists an account titled "Not signed in" (with Profiles: Work Max) instead of its name, and the header still shows "7 to clean up" while the rail says "No sessions yet". All captures were re-rendered.

Weak

  • all-statuses-*: the error state is amber in the rail but red in the session header, so it reads like a warning beside "Probably stuck".
  • honest-empty-390x844-*: the "We found Claude Code on this computer." chip wraps with its icon alone on a line.
  • all-statuses-390x844-*, ready-home-390x844-*, honest-empty-390x844-*: three-row header (nav on two lines); Needs you rows cut status and names to a few letters; in ready-home-390x844-* the sign-in need's two-line detail still ends cut ("…about thirty second…").
  • ready-home-*: a runway entry is titled "Not signed in" rather than by its account name; "7 to clean up" shows while the rail says "No sessions yet".

05-session-panel

05-session-panel: a session's page with its terminal, where it runs and its runway, at four widths in both themes

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

At 1024 to 1440 px the page is well ordered: title, status chip and account chip, a meta line, tabs, then the terminal beside the Where it runs and Runway cards, which stand a step above the canvas; the terminal is a true black panel in both themes with the SF Mono-style text of the DOM renderer, and after the second pass its black runs to the card's edges: the lighter (dark) or white (light) frame around it is gone, and the padding is black, so the text no longer sits flush against the edge of the black. "Switch account…" and "Relaunch" are quiet secondary buttons, so there is no competing violet in the panel. The top bar stays one row, and the lighter dark accent shows on the logo mark, the rail's New session button and the terminal cursor. The rail's selected row now reads "Stopped with an error" with no cut age; the session status still reads red in the header but amber for the same state in the rail beside it.

At 390 px the cleanup pill is now a broom and "7", but the header is still three rows and the page header still breaks: populated-390x844-* have no session title, leave a stray thin glyph before "Switch account…", and shrink the account chip to a dot with no name. The terminal now fills its card at 390 too, but its preview line still wraps mid-word ("live te / rminal)"), and the footer "Updated 1s ago · from this computer's process monitor" still wraps into two ragged columns. All captures were re-rendered; the second pass changed only the terminal's frame.

Weak

  • populated-390x844-*: no session title; a stray glyph before "Switch account…"; account chip shows only a dot.
  • populated-390x844-*: terminal text wraps mid-word; footer wraps into two columns.
  • populated-1280x800-*, populated-1440x900-*: the same error state is red in the header and amber in the rail.

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

Its copy still contradicts itself: the top card says "No token counts for it yet" while Tokens / min shows "406 a minute" and Context "6,000 tokens"; Errors reads "1 errors / 12 turns"; and Session cost is priced "for Opus" while the Model card says sonnet-5. session-header-actions opens the header's more menu with icons, disabled items explained underneath ("It has no conversation to carry over yet") and a red "Stop this session" last; the menu is now wide enough that "Continue on another account…" shows whole beside its ⌘⇧H shortcut. instance-overview-populated and session-overview have clear raised cards and a black terminal; after the second pass the terminal's black reaches its card's edge (the lighter frame around it is gone) and its text sits on a black margin instead of at the edge, as it does in session-header-actions too; in session-overview the rail is scrolled so its search field and tabs are out of view and the sacrificial header is a cut sliver at the top. 11-renewal

11-renewal: an account's page, meant to show its renewal date in each state (countdown, warning, danger, date only, unknown), the editor, and the chip on the accounts list

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

The account captures are framed on their subject again: each scrolls to the Settings card, where Plan and renewal shows a green "signed in" chip and the plan, a renewal line, the "Renewal date (optional)" field with Clear, "Entered by you · AI Juggler does not read billing" and a Flat fee field. Each state reads differently: account-countdown says "Renews in 10 days" in plain text, account-warning an amber "Renews in 6 days" chip, account-danger a red "Renewal date passed" chip, account-date-only "Renews 2024-11-02" and account-unknown "Expiry: unknown" with an empty mm/dd/yyyy field and a greyed Clear. The chips are calm and colour-coded, and the field sits right under its state. Two things are off: date-only writes the date as 2024-11-02 while the field beside it shows 11/02/2024, and unknown says "Expiry" where every other state says "Renews" or "Renewal". editor-1440x900 is the same image as account-warning, and editor-1280x800 is that state at a smaller width, so the editor is shown filled but never mid-edit. accounts-row-chip-1440x900 now shows the amber "Renews in 6 days" chip at the right of the Work Max row on the Accounts list. Above the Settings card, a cut sliver of the Sessions list and an empty card bottom fill the top of every account capture. All captures were re-rendered.

Weak

  • account-date-only-1440x900: "Renews 2024-11-02" beside a field showing 11/02/2024; two date formats.
  • account-unknown-1440x900: "Expiry: unknown" uses a different word from the other states' "Renews".
  • editor-1440x900: identical to account-warning-1440x900; no edit in progress is shown.
  • account-*, editor-*: the top of the panel is a cut list and an empty card bottom above the Settings card.

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

summary-dialog-* say what happened in one bold line ("2 sessions picked up again, 1 helper left behind, 1 process could not be checked and was left alone.") followed by a grey paragraph and "Got it"; it is readable, though the paragraph is long and small for a dialog that greets the user after a crash. cleanup-readopted-* mark the picked-up rows with a violet "Picked up after restart" state and keep the Automatic cleanup card underneath, all well aligned; in dark the Work Max toggle that is on now shows the lighter accent. adopted-panel-* are framed on their subject again: under the "Running — picked up after restart" notice the Processes tab lists the session's tree (claude "Picked up after restart", node (mcp-server-filesystem), npm, node (vite) on :5180, esbuild, node (tsc)) with Stop… buttons and sparklines, so the header's "picked up after restart" chip is backed by rows. The second pass re-spaced the Processes table at 1440: Process takes more width, and State, CPU, Memory, Ports and Age move right. That lets the node (vite) owner line show more ("probably started by subagent 'Run the f… · probably a dev server", only the subagent name still cut), and a later fix widened State to 11rem, so in cleanup-readopted "Left behind while closed" and "Picked up after restart" end with a clear gap before "0.2 %" / "0.7 %", and in adopted-panel before "5 %". Weak

  • cleanup-readopted-1440x900-*: the row actions don't line up: Clean up (4)… and Go to session end further right than the Stop… buttons.
  • summary-dialog-*: a long small grey paragraph in the first dialog after a restart.

27-ranking

27-ranking: how accounts are ranked for new work: the Home queue, the bell's notifications, and the New session dialog's Ranked for you list with its Why this order? explanation

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

In notification the bell popover is a clear raised panel in both themes, but every one of its five items still carries an equally solid violet button, so nothing leads, and the same action is labelled two ways ("Continue elsewhere" and "Continue on another account…"); at 1280x800 the last item's "Continue on another account…" button still runs flush into the popover footer's top border, its bottom edge sliced. In ranked and why the New session dialog groups well (folders as cards, a ranked list with a Suggested chip, then Which account? and a solid violet "Launch on Work Max"); in dark the Suggested chips, the Which account? ring and the "Why this order?" focus ring now carry the lighter violet. The layout problems are unchanged: at 1280x800 the Which account? card is cut at the bottom of the scroll area in ranked and pushed out of sight in why, in why-1440x900 its second line is sliced, the suggested account reads "82% headroom" in the ranked list but "58% left, from its latest session" in Which account?, and "82% left this week ·" ends on a stray separator. The queue captures are now re-rendered on the 0.3.2 Home: a violet "Work Max has room again" banner with a solid "Open feat/read-model" button, a Needs you card that leads with Personal and its solid "Open its page", Runway by account with bars and reset times, and Pick up where you left off. The banner button and "Open its page" are two solid primaries one above the other, and the banner button carries a focus ring in every queue capture, so it reads as already pressed.

Weak

  • notification-1280x800-dark.png, notification-1440x900-light.png: five equal solid violet buttons in one popover; two different labels for the same continue action.
  • notification-1280x800-dark.png, notification-1280x800-light.png: the last notification's "Continue on another account…" button sits flush on the popover footer's border, its bottom edge cut.

28-switch

28-switch: switching a conversation to another account: the "Continue on another account" list, the 3-step switch dialog's Confirm step in its variants, the running and failed states, and the notes and switch-anyway prompts

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

The Confirm step has a sound reading order in both themes: step chips, a large "Ready to switch to Personal" title, a short bullet summary, an amber Before you switch notice, then the stop-the-original radios with "recommended" in green, and the dialog panel stands well off the dimmed page in dark. The footer primaries ("Switch now", "Retry", "Switch anyway") are now solid violet, so the main action clearly outweighs Back and Cancel in every Confirm, notes, switch-anyway and failed capture; in dark the radio, checkbox and Confirm chip carry the lighter violet. At 800x600 the page behind the dialogs now keeps its top bar on one row, where the first pass had it wrapping. The long variants still clip: confirm-account-steps and confirm-set-up at 1280x800 end mid-section at the scroll edge (in confirm-set-up the github MCP row is sliced), confirm-token-steps opens scrolled so the title is gone (at 1280x800 now also the "Personal doesn't have 2 things" line, so release-notes sits straight under the step chips) and at 800x600 the github row's own heading is above the top edge. After the second pass the token command wraps at spaces ("--scope user", "user github" no longer split mid-word), but it still breaks at hyphens: "github --" / "scope user" and "server-" / "github"]" (the "-y" stays whole here); dialog-stop-800x600 cuts the selected option's hint under the footer. targets-out-of-room shows the "Continue on another account" dialog over the session title (cut to "feat/"), with no dimmed backdrop in dark, and every option carries the same amber Beta chip, so the warning colour stops meaning anything. failed says "resume it on Work where you left it" (the account is Work Max) yet offers only Cancel and Retry, and executing lists five steps with no mark of which one is running. In the other captures only the dimmed page behind changed: its terminal's black now reaches the card edge.

Weak

  • confirm-token-steps-800x600-dark.png, confirm-token-steps-800x600-light.png: opens scrolled; the github row's checkbox and name are above the visible area.
  • confirm-token-steps-*.png: the command block still breaks at hyphens ("github --" / "scope user", "server-" / "github"), so the flag and the package name read as split; mid-word wraps are fixed.
  • confirm-set-up-1280x800-*.png, confirm-account-steps-1280x800-*.png: the last set-up row is cut at the scroll edge.
  • dialog-stop-800x600-*.png: the selected "Stop it now and switch" hint is cut by the footer.
  • confirm-set-up-800x600-*.png: the set-up section the capture is named for is entirely below the fold.
  • targets-out-of-room-1280x900-*.png: no backdrop in dark; the dialog covers the session title; the same amber Beta chip on all three accounts; in Codex and Personal "Not enough use to forecast" sits right-aligned on its own line, leaving a gap.
  • failed-1280x800-*.png, failed-800x600-*.png: "resume it on Work" names the account wrongly, and there is no resume button.
  • executing-*.png: no current-step or done marks on the five steps.
  • notes-conflict-1280x900-*.png: "Before you switch" and "Set up on Personal" are two headings stacked with nothing between them.

0.3.5

On these captures Personal has no Claude app profile, so Desktop is greyed with why ("No desktop app profile on Personal yet…") and CLI is selected, tagged "Same as the original". Under them sits one coloured summary chip ("3 go along · 1 stays behind" amber, or "Everything goes along · 5 items" green) and one line on the original. The stop radios are one line each, with the examples behind "What does this mean?". The buttons read "Switch to CLI", or "Switch to CLI anyway" when Personal is out of room. At 800×600 the body scrolls under a fixed footer (Cancel, Back, Switch to CLI). executing lists its stages, the fourth saying where it starts: "Starting it in a terminal on Personal" (on Desktop: "Opening it in the Claude app on Personal"); failed is unchanged apart from the pills.

Weak (0.3.5)

0.3.2

Add an account (c-provider-cards, c-provider-cards-unchecked) is a tidy list of tool cards, each with its status after the name and one solid violet "Claude Code →", "Fake Desktop →" or "Codex →" on the right; Gemini CLI, not installed, gets a quieter "Install Gemini CLI →" link, and the unchecked state says "Found; couldn't check its version" in plain words. The dialog surface is a step lighter than the dimmed page in dark and white in light. The 390 problems inside the dialog are unchanged: the footer breaks, with "Launch on Work Max" wrapping onto two lines inside its button and "Launching in ai-juggler" wrapping beside it, and in e-launch-tight-390x844 the tight chip runs past the card to the dialog edge and is cut at "room le…". In e-launch-expanded the account rows' "Not measured yet ·" still leaves a dangling separator when the line wraps at 390. The second pass changed only the blurred rail rows behind the 390 dialogs; the dialogs, and their footer problems, are as they were ("Launching in ai-" / "juggler" still breaks at the hyphen). All captures were re-rendered.

Weak

  • e-launch-ok-390x844-*, e-launch-tight-390x844-*: "Launch on Work Max" wraps to two lines inside the button, and "Launching in ai-juggler" wraps next to it at the hyphen ("ai-" / "juggler").

39-import: bringing in a Claude Code folder, its problem states, and the sign-in step after

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

The page reads well at both sizes: a four-step tracker, a large "Bring in this folder?", a brighter lead sentence, the folder card (white on the grey canvas in light, a step lighter in dark), a Name field, then one solid violet "Bring it in" (or "Bring in settings only" in f-oversize) at the right with quieter Back and "Pick a different folder…" links. In dark the lighter accent now shows on the step-1 dot, "Bring it in", the "Sign in" button in f-signin and the Consumer Terms link; nothing else on these screens moved. f-non-config uses a red box and drops the primary, and f-oversize uses an amber box, so those two states are easy to read. The other problem states are not: in f-moved "That folder moved or is gone. Pick it again." is plain body text while the card still says "Signed in" and "Bring it in" stays the primary, and in f-duplicate the "Already imported as «Work»" line has its own Done button next to a still-primary "Bring it in", so two actions compete. In f-non-config the card still says its sign-in is kept in the macOS Keychain although the error says there is no sign-in. At 390 wide the step tracker wraps into three rows with stray connector lines, and in f-signin-390x844 the account name is cut to "cla…" while the row has room. All captures were re-rendered.

Weak

  • f-moved-*: the "moved or is gone" problem is plain text, not a warning, while "Bring it in" stays the solid primary and the card still says "Signed in".
  • f-duplicate-*: a Done button beside the duplicate notice competes with the primary "Bring it in".
  • f-non-config-*: the card's "Its sign-in is kept in the macOS Keychain…" contradicts the error "no settings, sign-in or session history in it".
  • f-*-390x844-*: the step tracker wraps into three rows with stray connector lines.
  • f-signin-390x844-*: the account name is cut to "cla…" though the row has space.
  • f-oversize-*-light: the amber notice turns a pinkish beige in light, close to f-non-config's error red.

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

So the first-pass problems stand.   • card-api_key-*: "API key account" appears twice, as the status and as a chip.

41-policy

41-policy: a profile's launch settings, its keys and passwords choice, and deleting launch settings

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), Settings' cards (98-local-cache) and the remove-account sheet. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good. First critique of this family: its captures were last taken at 0.1 and were re-rendered for 0.3.1 (D-60).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridLaunch settings sit in one card under their profile: name, model chip, Options, then delete and Launch on the right edge. Settings › Switching is one card of three choices, then the per-tool pickers.
HierarchyAccount, then profile, then "Launch settings · used when you start on this profile"; in Switching, "Your choice (all tools)" over its three lines, then "Per-tool choice".
SpacingThe settings card's rows keep the account card's rhythm; the env override row lines its fields up with the name above.
IconsDelete is the bin; Launch carries the terminal mark; the confirm has no icon, as the remove-account sheet.
Shared controlsThe shared Picker for "If a session crashes" and the per-tool choice (E28); radios with a hint under each label; the shared Modal for the confirm.
Disabled reasons"Save overrides" is greyed until a value changes.
Plain words"Hide them (recommended)", "Leave them in", "Ask on every switch"; the confirm: "It's removed from this account's launch settings and won't start new sessions. Sessions already made with it stay in History."
Type sizesNames 14 px, hints and labels 12–13 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×680Both; the folded rail at 1024 leaves the settings their width.

Good

  • Deleting launch settings asks first and says the one thing a user worries about: the sessions made with them stay. A refusal (an open session) is shown in the backend's own words, naming the session.
  • The keys and passwords choice says what each option means for the user, with the safe one marked as recommended.

Weak

  • The per-tool choice lists the preview's stand-in tool by its id ("fake-desktop"). Real tools show their names (providerDisplayName); only the preview's fixture lacks one.
  • The confirm says nothing about the profile's other launch settings, so deleting the last one leaves the profile with none and its Start disabled ("Add launch settings first"). That is said on the profile afterwards, not before.
  • accounts-pack-secrets-*: four solid violet buttons in one view at 1280 (New session, Add an account, and Launch on Deep work and on Quick fixes; three at 1024), so none of them reads as the page's primary.
  • accounts-pack-secrets-*: the disabled "Save overrides" under the env override is a pale violet that reads as pressed, not unavailable.
  • settings-pack-secrets-1024x680-dark: the page opens scrolled, so its "Switching" title is out of view above "Handovers and keys"; the page is only about 40 px taller than the window.
  • accounts-pack-secrets-1280x800-*: the rail rows read "Working · 748 d" and "Ended · 748 d ago"; the fixture's clock makes the ages look broken.

Fixed

  • 0.3.1 (D-60): one click on the bin deleted launch settings and, through the sessions table's cascade, every session made with them. It now asks first; the settings are marked removed, start nothing, and their sessions stay in History; while one is open the delete is refused, naming it. Vitest: delete-launch-settings.test.tsx; Rust: manage_accounts.rs "deleting_launch_settings_keeps_their_sessions_and_waits_for_open_ones".

Captures

CaptureWhat is on it
accounts-pack-secrets-1280x800, -1024x680Work Max's profile with its launch settings open: Deep work (opus) with an override CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS = 8000 and "If a session crashes: As in Settings", Quick fixes (sonnet).
settings-pack-secrets-1280x800, -1024x680Settings › Switching › Handovers and keys: Hide them (recommended), Leave them in, Ask on every switch; not chosen yet; the per-tool choice for each tool.
accounts-delete-launch-settings-1280x800, -1024x680"Delete Deep work?" over the Accounts page: it's removed from the launch settings and starts nothing new; its sessions stay in History; Cancel · Delete.
accounts-delete-launch-settings-refused-1280x800, -1024x680AJ-E2907: Delete pressed while three of Deep work's sessions are open: the same dialog says in red "«Deep work» has 3 sessions open: feat/read-model. Stop them first, then delete the launch settings", nothing is removed, and Close is the only button.

0.3.2

The dark Accounts page (accounts-pack-secrets) separates its layers: the Work Max account card stands off the canvas and the Launch settings box sits a step lighter inside it, and in light the white card has a soft edge on the grey canvas. The lighter dark accent shows on New session, Add an account and both Launch buttons, which makes the remaining problem plainer: four solid violet buttons in one view at 1280, so there is no single primary and Launch competes with Add an account. The disabled "Save overrides" is still a pale violet that reads like a pressed button rather than an unavailable one. Settings at 1024 (settings-pack-secrets-1024x680-dark) had its "Switching" page title back after the first pass, but after the second pass it opens scrolled again: the page starts at "Handovers and keys" with the "Switching" title scrolled out of view (the page is only about 40 px taller than the window, and the capture shows its end, with the footnote whole), so nothing on screen names the page except the selected Switching row in the side list; the three option boxes under "Your choice (all tools)" are a step apart from their card, the hints under each label are readable, and the per-tool pickers still list the preview tool as "fake-desktop". The rail at 1280 now shows "Stopped with an error" whole, without the cut-off age, but the fixture's other rows read "Working · 748 d" and "Ended · 748 d ago", an age no real session has. The delete confirm keeps its plain wording with a red-tinted Delete. All captures were re-rendered; the second pass changed only settings-pack-secrets-1024x680-dark.

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

The Fleet page reads in clear blocks: title with a Beta chip, a brighter intro sentence, the Link card, Paired machines, This Mac, and the Figurehead "Cloud" line; in dark every card stands off the canvas, and in light they are white on grey. Status chips carry the state ("Connected · mTLS · v1.0" green, "Degraded · v1.1" amber, "Offline" grey), the connection banners in connection-banners are ringed in amber, trust-broken puts its warning in its own amber-edged card with "Reset trust…", and the unpair confirm uses a red-tinted Unpair. All captures were re-rendered.

Weak

  • paired-1280x800-*, unfinished-1280x800-*: Office iMac's name wraps and its fingerprint stacks one group per line in a tall narrow column.
  • paired-1024x680-*: Office iMac wraps and its Unpair button drops to a line of its own.

  • this-mac-*, trust-broken-*: the top bar has no Fleet item while the other states show it as the current page.
  • connection-banners-*, paired-*, this-mac-*, trust-broken-*: the disabled "Add a machine…" is a pale violet that reads as pressed, not unavailable.

43-pairing

43-pairing: the Add a machine and Join a machine dialogs over Fleet, from code and fingerprint check to permissions, done and every failure

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

Every step reads as one clear dialog over a dimmed Fleet page: the six-digit code in 1-host-find and host-new-code is the biggest thing on screen, the Find → Verify → Permissions stepper ticks off as you go, and the primary action (Connect, Fingerprints match, Pair, Done, Start over, Turn pairing back on, Try again) is one solid violet fill in both themes, clearly ahead of the outlined Cancel or Close. In light the white dialog lifts well off the grey canvas, and in dark the dialog surface is a visible step above the page, so nothing blurs together. Since the first pass the dark captures carry the lighter accent on the stepper's current step, the address field's focus ring in 1-join-address, the Link toggle and the page's Add a machine…, and the rail behind at 1280 now shows "Stopped with an error" whole instead of cutting its age. The failure dialogs (fail-cancelled, fail-disabled, fail-expired, fail-locked, fail-mismatch) are calm and short, but their title ("The code didn't match" and the like) is still plain body-weight text with no warning mark or colour, and the stepper still shows 1–2–3 as if nothing happened. In fail-none-found the disabled Connect is a pale violet that reads as pressed rather than unavailable. All captures were re-rendered.

Weak

  • fail-cancelled, fail-disabled, fail-expired, fail-locked, fail-mismatch: the failure title has no warning mark or colour, and the stepper gives no hint of where it stopped, so a failure looks like an ordinary step.
  • fail-none-found: "No machines found…" sits in a bordered box styled like the machine picker, so it reads as a row you could select rather than a message.
  • 4-done: the success line is a plain "✓ Paired with Laptop" in body text; it is the end of the journey and could carry the success colour.
  • fail-none-found: the disabled Connect in the footer is a pale violet that reads as pressed, not unavailable.

45-remote

45-remote: a remote session or machine on Mac mini seen from Fleet: Overview live, stale and offline, Processes, and a view-only Actions tab

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

The header reads cleanly (breadcrumb, Work Max with Working and Mac mini chips, then a freshness line), and the Overview field card sits a step lighter than the canvas in dark and white on grey in light, with labels dimmer than values as intended. The freshness line carries the state: amber for Stale and Offline, but Live has only a grey dot, so the good state looks like the neutral one. In instance-actions-view-only the only action, Stop on Mac mini, is disabled as a pale red outline that is hard to read in light, while the reason sits beside it in body text. The data still contradicts itself: instance-processes says "Processes 3" right above "Unavailable — Mac mini hasn't reported this session's processes", and instance-overview-stale and -offline label every field "measured 1 s ago" while the header says the last update was 40 s ago or the Mac is offline. The design fixes show only in the chrome: at 1440 the rail's error rows now read "Stopped with an error" whole and the Personal row "Limit reached · 0% left" without a trailing cut, the top bar stays on one row, and in dark the Overview / Processes / Actions tab highlight and the logo mark take the lighter violet. Every capture was re-rendered.

Weak

  • instance-processes: "Processes 3" and "Unavailable — Mac mini hasn't reported this session's processes" contradict each other.
  • instance-overview-stale, instance-overview-offline: fields say "measured 1 s ago" under a Stale (40 s) or Offline header.
  • instance-actions-view-only (light): the disabled Stop on Mac mini is pale red on pale pink and barely readable.
  • instance-overview-live, instance-processes, machine-overview: the Live dot is grey, the same as a neutral state.

But Restore is still only a violet-outlined button, barely stronger than Cancel; the solid violet primaries of the switch dialogs did not reach it. In refused, the error line still breaks a word ("does not m / atch its checksum"), starts with a raw "invalid restore:" prefix, and has no colour beyond a ⚠ on "Nothing was restored", so a damaged backup looks like a routine note. Every page capture still opens scrolled, with the retention card's first line sliced at the top.

Weak

  • refused-1280x800-dark/light: the error breaks "match" across two lines and has no red or amber treatment; "invalid restore:" is a raw internal prefix.
  • plan-1280x800-dark/light: Restore, which replaces this Mac's data, is an outline (violet-edged) hardly stronger than Cancel, not the solid primary the switch dialogs now use.
  • done, progress and refused (dark/light): the page opens scrolled, with the retention card's first sentence sliced under the top edge.

68-accounts

68-accounts: Accounts, Usage, the limit decision, and an account on its credits

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). Written at 0.3.2 for the four new captures of the maintainer's report: a Codex account whose week is used up while its pre-bought credits carry the work on. Before 0.3.2 it was called "out of room". The family's other captures were not re-taken this round, and this critique doesn't judge them.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThe account card keeps its two gauges and the 7-day chart. The credits line sits between "Limits read" and the chart, in the card's own column. In the switch list, the credits words sit where a row's room always sits, under the badge and the forecast.
Hierarchy"Using credits · 14,150 left" is the row's medium-weight line. What it means for a session ("…goes on with its credits until the window resets Tue 7:13 AM") is the quieter line under it.
SpacingSame rhythm as the low and out-of-room lines (text-label, 2 px between the two lines). Nothing new is boxed.
IconsNone added. The week's ring stays at 0%, but its empty track is amber, not red: the window is used up and the work isn't stopped. The account above it, at 0% with no credits, keeps its red track.
Shared controlsNo new controls. The switch row is the same button, "Suggested" stays on the account with room (Personal), and Continue on another account… is unchanged.
Disabled reasonsNone. An account on its credits can still be picked.
Plain words"Using credits · 14,150 left" (a whole number with separators, never "14150.1635025000"). "Used up, on credits · back in 2 days" under the gauge, where it said "Limit hit". "Sessions on it go on with its credits until the window resets …". No "out of room" for it anywhere.
Type sizes12–13 px labels as the rest of the row; nothing under 12 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light: the credits words are ink, not warning or danger, and read clearly on both.
1280×800 and 1024×7001280×800 captured; 1024 checked by hand in the preview (the switch list and the card wrap without clipping).

Good

  • The switch list now has three honest states side by side: Personal with room (Suggested), Codex on its credits (says it goes on until the reset), and Fake Desk at 0 credits ("Out of room until Tue 7:13 AM", red, unchanged).
  • The ranking's own words agree with the row ("using its credits · quiet"), so the explanation and the room line never contradict.
  • The amber track tells the card's two 0% rings apart at a glance, without a new mark.

Weak

  • The switch row on credits is the tallest in the list: badge, forecast, two credits lines, the "Doesn't carry" line and the factors. In the Continue on another account dialog all three rows still fit at 1280×800.
  • "using its credits" in the factors repeats the room line above it. It's kept because it explains the order, but it's the second time the row says it.
  • The card's ring still reads "0%". The words beside it carry the credits truth; the ring alone doesn't.
  • accounts-credits-1280x800 (both themes): the page opens scrolled, so the Fake Desk card's header (name and email) is cut under the top bar.

Fixed

  • The maintainer's report (0.3.2): the card and the switch list said "Limit hit" and "Out of room until …" for a Codex account that kept working on 14,150 credits. Both now say it's using its credits, and what that means for a session.

69-rail-narrow

69-rail-narrow: the narrow window

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThe folded column centres every mark on one axis.
HierarchyThe Group and View door first, then the needs count, then the places.
SpacingMarks evenly spaced; the bottom actions grouped.
IconsEvery mark is an app's icon with its profile badge; the door is View's own icon.
Shared controlsOpened, the list has the shared switch and View.
Disabled reasonsNot applicable.
Plain wordsEach icon names itself on hover and to screen readers.
Type sizesThe needs count 12 px; nothing smaller.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×700Below 1100 px the list folds; 860 and 390 captured.

Good

  • The folded column keeps a Group and View door that opens the list where the switch and View are (⌘B too).

Weak

  • At 390 px the icons-only rail is the whole navigation; fine for a desktop minimum.
  • At 390 px the header still takes two rows (the tabs wrap after Accounts; Usage and History share the second row with the actions), about 120 px before the page starts.
  • opened-860x700 (both themes): with the list open, the header still wraps its actions (broom pill, New session, bell, avatar) onto a second row under the tabs.
  • folded-390x844 (both themes): the Needs you rows cut their status to "Limit reach…" and "Stopped wi…".
  • folded-390x844 (both themes): the first need's two-line detail breaks after a separator, so its first line ends in a dangling "·" ("storefront · AI Juggler ·").

Fixed

  • Re-render: below 1100 px the folded rail hid the grouping switch and View; the only way back was the logo or ⌘B. The door is at the top of the column now.
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): the opened list's top is a shorter stack — search and View 28 px, the grouping switch 30 px (each segment still a 24 px target), less air before Needs you — so the first session sits 13 px higher, every control where it was.
  • Second re-render: the opened list at 860 shows the shorter top (search and View 28 px, the switch 30 px) and Needs you starting higher; the folded column is unchanged. Nothing else to fix here.

76-keys: the keyboard shortcuts sheet and the folded rail on a session page

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

sheet-1280x800 lays the shortcuts out in two columns (Anywhere, Screens) with key caps right-aligned on thin rules; it is easy to scan, and the dark sheet sits a step above the dimmed page. After the design fixes the close button's focus ring is in the lighter dark accent (sheet, both sizes). After the second pass the sheet is wider and ends in a "More below ⌄" chip when its list runs past the window: at 1024x680 the list stops at "Close a sheet, menu or search" with the chip under it, so "Open a row's menu" and "Open a session's page" are signposted rather than silently hidden. At 1280x800 the chip shows too, under "Open a session's page (on its row)", although the window has room left below the sheet; in both sizes the last visible key cap (Esc, ↩) is still slightly clipped at the bottom of the list's scroll area. folded-1024x680 shows the session page with the rail folded to marks, and folded-1280x800 the full page; the "Where it runs" and Runway cards lift off the canvas in both themes, and the terminal's black now fills its card to the edges in both themes (no white frame in light, no lighter frame in dark), with the text inset from the black's edge. The rail in folded-1280x800 now reads "Stopped with an error" whole, without the cut-off age, and the logo mark and the rail's New session outline take the lighter accent.

0.4.1

The maintainer found the right column's key caps cut off at the sheet's edge in the app ("⌘0", "⌘⇧P", "⌘F" half hidden): WebKit's fonts and a visible scrollbar took the room Chromium left. The sheet is now 48rem wide (Modal broad), its columns are as many as fit at 19rem each (one when narrow), labels wrap and key caps never shrink, and the list keeps a stable scrollbar gutter, so a scrollbar never covers a key. sheet-1024x680 and sheet-1280x800 (both themes) show two columns with a clear gap right of every key cap; the list still scrolls and still ends in "More below ⌄". Checked in WebKit too (the app's engine): at 1024 × 680 two columns and at 720 × 680 one, 0 of 54 key caps outside the list either way.

Weak

  • sheet-1024x680, sheet-1280x800 (both themes): the last visible key cap (Esc at 1024, ↩ at 1280) is still slightly clipped at the bottom of the list above "More below".
  • sheet-1280x800 (both themes): the list scrolls ("More below") although the window has room for the sheet to grow.

  • the app's icon with the state's dot;
  • the title;
  • "● Claude Code · claude · 1 min ago" on line 2: the App's colour dot and name, then when. The project is left out on its own page;
  • the state word at the line's end.

Before, the state led the second line, and the App sat in a column of its own on wide windows.

**project-overview.** The tab count now reads 27 to match the list. The Open sessions card lists its rows in the order Needs you, Working, Open, with section headings when more than one is present.

CheckFinding
GridMarks sit in the rail's mark column; History's rows put tool, project and account marks in fixed columns.
HierarchyA project's colour and mark first, its name beside it.
SpacingThe rail's rhythm.
IconsReal icons only where they are real images; otherwise a coloured monogram.
Shared controlsThe mark sheet's choices are buttons in a grid.
Disabled reasonsNot applicable.
Plain wordsEach mark names its project on hover and to screen readers.
Type sizesMonograms 12 px and up.
Both themesChecked dark and light; every colour keeps its contrast.
1280×800 and 1024×700Captured at 1280; the folded column is 69-rail-narrow's.

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

collapsed, nothing and open show the Add a fresh account form with a styled "Start with the same settings as" chip and a solid violet "Create and sign in"; nothing explains in one line that Personal has nothing to copy. open and account-sheet list each item with its own checkbox and a plain explanation, and walk through the github token step as numbered instructions with a command and Copy button. After the second pass the command block wraps at spaces, so words and flags like "--scope", "user" and "command" stay whole, but it still breaks at hyphens: open-* split the JSON as '["-' / 'y",' and "server-" / "github", and account-sheet-* split "github --" / "scope user" and "server-" / "github". Weak

  • account-sheet-dark/light, open-dark/light: the command block still breaks at hyphens ('["-' / 'y",', "server-" / "github", "github --" / "scope"), so the pasted line is hard to check by eye.
  • account-sheet-dark/light, open-dark/light: the Copy button under the command is cut by the scroll edge; only its top border shows.

88-subagents

88-subagents: a session's Subagents tab and a subagent's page

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThe tab is a table: Subagent · State · Time · Tokens · Model, groups as header rows on the same columns.
HierarchyThe summary line first ("10 subagents · 3 running · 1 failed · 38 % of this session's tokens"), then the views, then the groups.
SpacingRows on the table's rhythm; the search stretches so the filter bar has no gap at 1280.
IconsThe state pills carry their marks; the subagent page's cards use the app's card titles.
Shared controlsRunning · Needs attention · All is the shared Segmented; Type and Sort are the shared Picker.
Disabled reasonsType: All types is disabled with its reason when every subagent is one type.
Plain words"You asked: …" heads a group; "Its prompt isn't recorded by Claude Code".
Type sizesRows 14 px, kinds and times 12–13 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×700At 1024 the page grows long; the detail's right column ends early at 1280.

Good

  • Summary line first; Running · Needs attention · All; grouped under the message that started them; tree lines only for who started whom.
  • A subagent's page has its task, result, work, usage and processes as cards, and says honestly what the tool doesn't record.
  • The 437-subagent session opens grouped and windowed.

Weak

  • Type: All types shows even when every subagent is one type.
  • Detail cards at 1280 put Usage and Processes in a right column that ends long before the left.
  • tab-*-1280x800, detail-*-1280x800, row-count-1280x800 (both themes): the rail opens scrolled past its search, switch and Needs you header.
  • tab-most-tokens-1280x800 (both themes): one row under "6 subagents" with All selected; the sort looks like a filter.

Fixed

  • Re-render: no "├" on first-level rows (lines only show who started whom); the breadcrumb starts with the session's title, not "This session"; an empty tab shows no dead filter bar and says "This session hasn't started any subagents".
  • 0.3 (this branch): the old tab's capture (session-subagents) was retired; the tab is tab-*.

Captures

CaptureWhat is on itWhat changed in 0.3

0.3.2

The subagents table is a raised card on the darker canvas (white on grey in light), and the state pills (Running cyan, Done green, Failed red, each with its mark) stay readable in both themes; the failed reason in detail-failed is a red-bordered box that reads in both. The search field stretches, so tab-all-grouped, tab-running and tab-none-match line the controls up edge to edge. After the design fixes the dark accent is lighter: it shows on the logo mark, New session, the selected Subagents or Usage tab, tab-search's focused field ring, usage-line's Input bar and dot and the "see which ›" link, a little brighter on the dark cards; the light captures are unchanged apart from the header. The detail pages still end their right column (Usage, Processes) long before the left one in detail-done and detail-failed. On every tab-* and detail-* capture at 1280 the rail is still scrolled so its search box, the App/Project/Account switch and the Needs you header are out of view and the first row is "Personal · Limit reached", which makes the rail look headless. row-count shows its subject again: the fifth Open now row carries "+3 agents" beside Working, and after the second pass's last fix the row cuts the project and keeps the age: "ai-… · 1 h ago · +3 agents"; the same headless rail applies. The second pass otherwise only touches the rail here: the Probably asks you row reads "ai-j… · 3 h ago" on every tab-* and detail-* capture. tab-most-tokens still shows a single row under a group that says "6 subagents" with All selected, so the sort reads like a filter. Only row-count has an earlier committed render to compare against; the tab-*, detail-* and usage-line captures are committed for the first time in this round.

0.3.4: the Overview's switcher, and the summary that adds up

The maintainer, testing 0.3.3: "a quick way to switch conversation of a session between subagents, so I can easily and quickly drill in", matching how the tools' own apps show them; the Subagents tab stays the full list.

CheckFinding
GridAt 1280 the switcher is a 12 rem column on the conversation's left; the conversation keeps the rest (about 415 px), the side column its 340 px. Narrower than 576 px, the list sits above the conversation in a short scrolling box, never clipped.
Hierarchy"This session" first, chosen; then Running · 3, Started from here · 1, and Done · 7 folded: what runs, what it started, then history.
SpacingRows on the rail's rhythm: a dot, a two-line title, its kind or time under it.
IconsA live dot pulses on running ones (still under reduced motion), a red one on a failed ended one; no new icons.
Shared controlsBack is the shared Button, the same as the Subagents tab's conversation; Done is a disclosure like the work row's.
Plain words"This session · Its terminal", "Running · 3", "Started from here · 1", "Done · 7", "All 10 in Subagents ›".
Type sizesTitles 13 px medium, their second lines 12 px muted, section heads 12 px semibold muted.
Both themesChecked dark and light; axe clean on both shots.
The summary line"· 2 with no recorded end" beside "1 failed": the line adds up to Needs attention 3.
Good
  • One click from the conversation to a running subagent's, Esc back; the terminal is hidden, never rebuilt, and keeps its place.
  • What the native apps list beside a session is here in the same order of importance: running now, the sessions it started, then what ended.
Weak
  • At 1280 the conversation pane narrows to about 415 px while the switcher shows; a wider window gives it more. Folding the side column when the switcher is open is a 0.4 question (AJ-E3106).
  • A started session shows only while its app is open: the app's own record is read from its running instances, as its sidebar is.

0.3.5

The maintainer on 0.3.4's Overview column: "you need to be clearer what is this session, and what are subagents … right now it is not clear that what you are seeing is subagents. Why not use lines and branch graphics like Claude does."

**switcher-own, switcher-subagent.** The column is a tree now. At the top, the root: a ring mark, "This session", and under it "Its terminal" (or "Its main conversation"). A line runs down from it; each kind of conversation hangs off it on an elbow: "Subagents running · 3", "Sessions it started · 1", "Finished subagents · 7" (folded). Each of them has its own elbows to its leaves, and each leaf a mark for its kind: a stack for a subagent (with a live dot while it runs, red when it failed), a chat mark for a session it started. The group names say what they hold; their tooltips add "helpers working for it now" and "separate sessions, each with its own page". The lines are the faint hairline colour, so they guide without competing with the names.

Weak

  • On a narrow page the tree sits in its own small scrolling box above the conversation, where the lines take a little of the width.

0.4.1 (E28, 2026-11-07)

What changed in these captures, each looked at:

  • **usage-line-1024x700** (both themes): the line reads "Subagents: 38% of this session's tokens · see which ›", with "Used most:" naming the three that used most, each a link (AJ-E2812 R1). The second name truncates at its column; it reads, but the gap before its "288k" is wider than the others'.
  • **detail-done-1280x800** (both themes): the Processes card no longer offers "Stop it from the session's Processes tab" for a subagent that ran no commands. With commands it says "See the session's processes ›" when none matched, and "Stop one from the session's Processes tab ›" when one did.
  • **detail-running-1280x800 and 1024x700**: the head says how long it has run ("Running · 9m"); the breadcrumb goes back one step at a time (Esc), the spec walking it twice.
  • **Every capture**: the header's ‹ › Back and Forward and the rail's App headers ("Per… Claude Code") come from other 0.4.1 work, not this family's.

Weak, not this family's to fix: at 1280 the rail cuts its App names to "Per…" and "Wor…" beside "Claude Code", and the header's room pill shrinks to "P…". row-count (both themes) fails axe on a RoomChip with an aria-label its role doesn't allow (c71c77e2d).

90-processes

90-processes: the Processes page, what sessions and apps run and left behind, ports, cleanup history, automatic cleanup and stopping, at 1280, 1680 and 2000

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

The four count tiles (7 to clean up in amber, 1 didn't stop in red, 1 waiting, 24 running) and the process table stand as raised cards in both themes, and the table keeps a clear hierarchy: app group rows, session rows with a dimmer summary, then processes with coloured state words and marks (amber Left behind, red Didn't stop, cyan Working) and small CPU sparklines. The design fixes show here only in dark, as the lighter accent: the logo mark, the New session buttons in the top bar and the rail, the "Left running" pills on Ports, the on switch for Work Max's "Tidy left-behind dev servers" (ended-*, history-*, rules-preview) and the selected radio in the stop dialog are a brighter violet; the light captures are unchanged apart from sub-pixel shifts in the top bar, where session-tab-1680x1000 keeps "This Mac · Plenty of room" and the whole "7 to clean up" pill on one row. At 1280 the Owner column drops; after the second pass the columns are re-spaced, so group summaries are whole ("payments · 4 processes · 3.0 GB · 3 %") and Ports sits a clear gap from Memory, and with State now 11rem wide "Left behind while closed" ends with a clear gap before the CPU figure in running-* and attention-* at 1280 in both themes. At 1680 and 2000 the only change is a wider gap before Ports; at 2000 the page is a centred column with wide gutters and its 12 px type looks small. In every light capture with Automatic cleanup (ended-*, history-*, ports-*, rules-preview) the off switches are still pale grey tracks on white that nearly vanish. other-apps opens with the table scrolled so the first visible row's top half sits under the sticky column header, the filters popover ends on its "App" heading with the options below it cut, and large's group row reads "2500 %" CPU, a sum that looks like an error. The stop dialogs use a coral (dark) or red (light) destructive button that names the PID and port ("Stop vite (PID 8123) and free :5173"), readable, but their radios still sit at the dialog's right edge, far from "This process only" and the other labels. The rail's status lines show whole ("Stopped with an error", "Probably stuck"), and after the second pass the Open now row keeps its age too: "ai-j… · 3 h ago Probably asks you" where it read "ai-jugg… · 3 …".

Weak

  • history-1280x800-light.png, ended-1280x800-light.png, ports-1680x1000-light.png, rules-preview-1680x1000-light.png: the off switches in Automatic cleanup are nearly invisible on white.
  • other-apps-1680x1000-dark.png, other-apps-1680x1000-light.png: a half-hidden row under the sticky table header.
  • filters-1680x1000-dark.png, filters-1680x1000-light.png: the Filters popover ends on its "App" heading with the options cut.
  • large-1680x1000-dark.png, large-1680x1000-light.png: "2500 %" CPU on the group row reads as a mistake.
  • stop-1680x1000-dark.png, stop-1680x1000-light.png: the What to stop radios sit at the dialog's right edge, away from their labels.

91-more-tools: adding accounts for more tools, the tool chooser and first run finding four setups

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

add-account-chooser lists four installed tools (Claude Code, OpenCode, Kimi Code, Grok Build), each with a solid violet "<tool> →" button; these are four equal primaries in one small dialog, so nothing says which is the usual pick, and an outlined button per row would carry the same choice more quietly. The dialog itself lifts well off the blurred page in both themes; in dark only its logo mark changed, to the lighter accent. first-run-found is a well-ordered page: a four-step header, a large heading ("You already have 4 setups on this computer."), the setups grouped by tool, then a colour dot, name and path per row, and two choice cards with "Use them where they are" marked Recommended and selected; in dark the step number, the four ticked checkboxes, the selected radio and the selected card's border are the lighter violet and stand out better against the near-black. At 1280 × 800 the capture still ends at "Add a fresh account instead · Import from another folder…", so the page's primary button is below the fold on first run. tools-on-this-mac (one capture per theme; it replaces the three identical grok-build-, kimi-code- and opencode-tool-page captures) shows Settings › Accounts: "Accounts and profiles" with Open Accounts, then "Tools on this computer" as one raised card with a row per tool (lettered mark, name, "1 account · Version … · not tested with AI Juggler yet"), OpenCode's second line "OpenCode 1.19.4 is out; … To update, run opencode upgrade yourself.", the ticked "Look for new versions of your tools once a day", and Renewal dates below. It reads cleanly in both themes, with the tool names bright and their facts a step dimmer, and its name now says what it shows. The tool rows still carry no chevron or button that would open a page of that tool.

Weak

  • add-account-chooser-1280x800-dark.png, add-account-chooser-1280x800-light.png: four solid violet buttons, no single primary.
  • first-run-found-1280x800-dark.png, first-run-found-1280x800-light.png: the button that brings the setups in is below the first screen.
  • tools-on-this-mac-1280x800-*: under Tools on this computer the rows are not doors; nothing on a row opens that tool's page or its accounts.
  • tools-on-this-mac-1280x800-*: the rail shows "Also: History ›" above "Nothing open yet", the footnote before the message.

91-pools

91-pools: the rail's Pool grouping, Move to pool, Agent access

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThe Pool grouping uses the rail's group rows; Move to pool is a list of pools in one bordered list.
HierarchySheet title, then the session ("ai-juggler · feat/read-model · Work Max"), then the pools.
SpacingThe rail's rhythm; the sheet's rows evenly spaced.
IconsThe sheet's pools have none, unlike the row menus' lines.
Shared controlsThe grouping is the shared Segmented; Agent access uses the shared Picker.
Disabled reasonsThe pool it's in says "It's here" instead of greying out.
Plain words"ai-juggler · all sessions" names a project's own pool.
Type sizesBody 14 px, hints 12 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×700Captured at 1280 only.

Good

  • The rail's Pool grouping appears once a pool of yours exists; a pool's header opens its page.

Weak

  • The switch shows four segments in a 264 px rail; it fits only because segments size to their words.
  • Move to pool's lines carry no icon or member count.

Fixed

  • Re-render: segments size by their words (Pool cut "Account" short).
  • 0.3 (this branch): the old pool page's captures (pool-cards, pool-access, pool-sheet) were retired; the pool's page is the canvas, in 97-pool-canvas.

92-session-pages

92-session-pages: a session's page, for each kind of session

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridHeader, tabs and the two columns (conversation, Where it runs and Runway) on the page's edges.
HierarchyTitle, then the state chip, then the facts line; Go to it is the one primary.
SpacingThis branch: one row of chips, so the header no longer wraps to a second chip row at 1280.
IconsThe app's icon with the profile's badge beside it; Go to it carries its icon and ↵.
Shared controlsNo system controls.
Disabled reasonsAn ended session offers Continue in AI Juggler and Copy resume command instead of a greyed Go to it.
Plain words"Asks you · 3 min", "Running in the Claude app · viewed 2 min ago", "Started outside AI Juggler: shown here to read and follow".
Type sizesChips and facts 13 px, body 14 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×700Captured at 1280, 1680, 2000 and 2000 at 200 %.

Good

  • One page for every kind of session: where it runs, its conversation's last turns, Go to it.
  • Where it runs says only what it knows (app, window, profile, account, folder).

Weak

  • Before this branch the header repeated itself: "Running in the Claude app", "Profile: Work Max" and the account and folder as chips, while the facts line and Where it runs said them again.
  • The Overview's footnote ("from the apps' own session files and the end of its transcript") is a lowercase fragment under the page; it reads as a sentence cut off. It is the app-wide "from …" source pattern, kept as it is.
  • The Account menu's first line is the plan's name ("Claude Max") under the heading Account, while the chip says the profile ("Work Max"); clear once open, but the chip and its menu lead with different names.
  • outside-working-1280x800 (dark): the "Continue on another account" card ends under the "More below" fade, which slices "Week resets Thu 2:13 AM" in half.
  • outside-asks-2000x1100-zoom200 (both themes): the conversation shrinks to the cut bottom of one line ("merge sort it out?") above the asks band.
  • usage-1280x800, usage-not-measured-1280x800: the chart legend (Input 5.3M, Output 1.2M; 1.3M, 282k) and By kind (6.2M, 1.4M; 1.4M, 301k) give different totals for the same session without saying why.
  • outside-steps-open-1280x800 (both themes): "Ran 2 commands" is still folded, so the capture named for opened steps shows none.
  • outside-asks, outside-steps-open, last-messages-off, account-menu-open: two solid violet Go to it buttons on one screen (header and the "Claude asks you" band).

Fixed

  • Re-render: the tool and the pool are named doors on the facts line (the tool was a bare mark).
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): the header has two chips, its state and one Account menu (the profile's badge and name ▾: Account, Profile, Folder and Open the profile's page). "Running in …" or "Last active …" is on the facts line after the tool. The separate Where, Profile, Account and Folder chips went; nothing they held is lost (the menu and Where it runs keep it) and no door moved: Go to it, the tool and the pool are where they were.
  • Second re-render: looked at again by eye at 1280, 1680 and 2000 in both themes: one chip row, the Account menu opens under its chip without covering the title, the facts line carries where it runs. Nothing else to fix here.

0.3.4

The maintainer, testing 0.3.3: the conversation showed every note written between steps, where the tools' own windows fold a turn's work; and white text on blue-grey was hard to read at length.

  • Every conversation capture (outside-asks at all four sizes, outside-working, outside-steps-open, own-overview, account-menu-open, last-messages-off, usage) now has the conversation card on the near-black reading surface (#0d0d0e, measured) under neutral text. The side cards keep their blue-grey, so the conversation reads as its own pane. In light the card is white with neutral greys.
  • A finished turn is the person's message, one quiet row ("Worked for 1 min · Ran 2 commands"), then the reply. outside-steps-open shows that row opened onto its steps, and the steps opened to their lines. The latest turn of a working session (outside-working) shows open as it goes.
  • "Claude asks you" quotes the question in the reply, never one from the notes.
  • Weak: at 200 % zoom (outside-asks-2000x1100-zoom200) the conversation is still a sliver above the asks band, as in 0.3.2.

0.4.1 (E21, 2026-11-07): re-shot

What changed, each looked at: the Conversation card's caption reads "From its transcript · not saved" (was "nothing kept": the turns are held in memory a minute after the page closes, never written, ADR-0024); D-62's names throughout (Claude app · Work Max, Account rows). Other moves (header ‹ ›, rail App headers) are other 0.4.1 work.

93-accounts-profiles

93-accounts-profiles: Accounts with one, two and six profiles

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good. Written at the 0.3 second re-render, the first time these captures were taken since 0.1 (the spec lives in session-pages-gallery.spec.ts).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridOne card per account on the page's width: its head (tool mark, colour dot, name, address · plan), its runway and 7-day chart, then one row per profile.
HierarchyAccount name, then its address and plan; "3 profiles share this account" on the head's right; profile rows below in smaller type.
SpacingRows on the 8 px rhythm; the chart band is the card's tallest part.
IconsThe tool's mark on each account, a lettered badge in the profile's colour on each profile ("Wo", "2", "CA").
Shared controlsOpen and ⋯ are the shared row actions; Add an account is the page's one primary.
Disabled reasons"Nothing open" beside Open instead of a greyed control.
Plain wordsThe account and profile explainer at the top; "No current reading. Limits come from the Claude app's meter while it's open on this profile…".
Type sizesBody 14 px, profile paths and hints 12–13 px; nothing under 12 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×7001280 only (the spec's size).

Good

  • The account versus profile distinction is said once at the top and then shown: one card, several profile rows, "3 profiles share this account".
  • Each card shows usage at a glance (the 7-day tokens chart) even before limits are measured.

Weak

  • "Work, Work 2 and 1 more" as an account's name is built from its profiles' names; the address under it is what tells two accounts apart.
  • With nothing open, the rail shows "Also: History ›" above "Nothing open yet", the footnote before the message.
  • The ring with "–" for an unmeasured window is a large empty mark; the words beside it ("Not measured yet") carry the meaning.
  • The explanation under the chart is one long line that runs to the card's edge at 1280, its link at the very end and cut to "Measure its limits…" (accounts-one-profile, accounts-two-profiles, accounts-six-profiles).

Fixed

  • Nothing in this family this round: the captures had not been re-taken since 0.1 (top bar with Home · Accounts · History · Settings · More, the old rail filters, two bars per window). They now show the 0.3 header, the rail's App · Project · Account switch and the account card with its runway ring and chart.

Captures

CaptureWhat is on itWhat changed since the last capture
accounts-one-profile-1280x800One account "Work" (o…@work.com · Max), not measured, 64M tokens in 7 days, one profile row "Work · Claude Code ~/…/w1".The 0.3 header and rail; the runway ring and 7-day chart replace two empty bars; the profile's folder is shortened to ~/…/w1.
accounts-two-profiles-1280x800"Work and Work 2" (o…@work.com · Max): 2 profiles share this account, 102M tokens in 7 days, rows Work (~/…/w1) and Work 2 (~/…/w2).Same.
accounts-six-profiles-1280x800Three accounts: "Work, Work 2 and 1 more" (Acme · Team, 3 profiles share it), "Personal and Weekend" (2), and one more below the fold.Same.

0.3.2

The account cards stand off the page in both themes: in dark the card is a clear step lighter than the near-black canvas, and in light the white cards with a soft shadow sit on the grey canvas, so the one-, two- and three-profile cards (accounts-one-profile, accounts-two-profiles, accounts-six-profiles) read as separate objects. Add an account is one solid violet fill and the obvious primary on the page; the rail's New session stays the pale secondary beside it. After the design fixes the dark captures carry the lighter accent on the logo mark, Add an account and the top bar's New session, which now read a touch brighter; the light captures changed only in the top bar, where "This Mac · Plenty of room" moved by a sub-pixel and the bar stays one row with the whole "7 to clean up" pill. The explainer sentence under "Accounts" and the "No current reading…" line are brighter than the dim labels ("Last 7 days, in and out", "Not measured yet"), so the sentence level is visible. The "No current reading…" line still runs the full card width at 1280 and ends in the truncated link "Measure its limits…", and the rail still puts "Also: History ›" above "Nothing open yet". In accounts-six-profiles the second account's card is cut by the fold, which is the scroll position, not a layout fault.

0.4.1 (E21, 2026-11-07): re-shot

What changed, each looked at: App rows say "Nothing open" or "n open now" and carry no folder path (design review #63); the header's ‹ › and the rail are other 0.4.1 work.

93-messages

93-messages: a pool's Messages and messages in Needs you

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThread list and open thread side by side on the panel's two columns.
HierarchyThread title, who is in it, then the messages; each bubble names its sender, kind and time.
SpacingBubbles evenly spaced; the "2 of 6 agent messages in a row without you" pill wraps inside its column.
IconsNeeds you's message cards carry the message mark; Pause and Stop are words.
Shared controlsNo system controls; the search is the app's field.
Disabled reasonsA paused pool says why with a banner and Resume.
Plain words"To Builder · Started its turn", "an agent", "Pause this pool's messages".
Type sizesBody 14 px, meta 12–13 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light; your own bubbles stand apart in both.
1280×800 and 1024×700Captured at 1280, 1680 and 2000; at 800 px the reply box is below the fold.

Good

  • Messages over the canvas use the page's width: list and thread side by side.

Weak

  • The thread's bubbles stay narrow even with the room; the reply box is below the fold at 800 px.
  • pool-paused-1280x800: Resume messages in the paused banner and New message below it are both solid violet, two primaries a few lines apart.
  • pool-docs-1280x800, -1680x1050, -2000x1250: the capture opens scrolled to the documents, so the thread above is cut at the panel's top edge (at 1280 only the reply box's "Deliver to their sessions" row and Send show; at 2000 a cut bubble, and a thread-list column empty except "Pause this pool's messages").
  • pool-messages-2000x1250, pool-docs-2000x1250: the open thread stops well short of the Messages card's right edge, leaving an empty strip.

Fixed

  • Re-render: the panel takes the page's width (it sat beside the inspector, a few words wide), and its pill wraps inside its column.
