94-places

94-places: Home and the rail with no, one, a typical and a busy set of sessions

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

Home at three loads (home-empty, home-typical with 8 open, home-busy with 40 open), the rail alone at 1, 8 and 40 sessions, and the rail's View menu (view), in both themes. The Your apps cards, the Needs you card and the Runway by account card stand a step off the canvas in dark and as white cards on grey in light, and the one primary per screen is solid violet (New session in home-empty, Open its page on the first Needs you row, Allow on the "Jump to a session's own window" prompt). The headline "5 sessions need you" with its "40 open · 1 inactive · 1 ended today" line gives a clear top level, and the amber "Asks you" / "Limit reached" states in the rail are readable in both themes. After the design fixes the dark captures carry the lighter accent on the logo mark, New session, Open its page, Allow and the "Measure its limits ›" links, all a touch brighter on the near-black; the light captures changed only in the top bar, which stays one row with "This Mac · Plenty of room" and the whole "7 to clean up" pill. In the Your apps row the other cards put their Open button on one line and the amber "1 needs you" chip on the line below; the ChatGPT card has no chip, so Open ChatGPT drops to that bottom line and sits lower than Open Claude and Open Terminal beside it (home-typical, home-busy). In home-busy the Work Max Claude and Terminal cards break their line as "Work Max ·" with "10 sessions" alone below, and in Needs you the fix/limits row, which has no ↗ icon, pushes its Open its page button to the right edge while the rows above and below keep theirs inset, so the buttons don't line up. In home-empty the Runway by account card still sits alone in the right-hand column with nothing beside it. The rail captures (rail-1, rail-8, rail-40) are the 264 px rail only; there the account names in group headers are cut to a few letters ("Claude · Pers…", "Claude · Work…") to make room for the warning count. After the second pass the rail rows keep their time whole and cut the project instead ("trip-not… · viewed 40 min ago" where it read "viewed 40 min a…"; "ai-ju… · viewed 2 min ago" in rail-1), and after a later fix a short project sits against its dot with no gap and keeps its time whole ("blog · 12 min ago") in home-typical, rail-8 and view. The new view capture opens the rail's filter button to a View menu: Show (Inactive "in an app's list, not running", Quiet ones, Ended today, Background runs, Subagents, Other Macs, Archived) and Only (App, Profile, Account, Project, each "All ›"), with a violet focus ring on Inactive; the hints are a quiet grey beside each name and it reads well in both themes, but the Show lines carry no tick or switch, so the menu doesn't say which of them are on, and it opens over the page heading "3 sessions need you" and the rail's Account segment.

0.4

Re-shot with 0.4's rail and words (D-62). The rail groups by App: rail-8 now shows four App groups (Claude Code · Work Max with its three terminal sessions in one group, ChatGPT, Claude app · Personal, Claude app · Work Max) where it showed five, each header with its App's dot, and a row's ↗ reads "Go to it in Claude app · Work Max". Rows carry their App's dot, never an Account colour; Inactive apps start folded. On Home (home-typical, home-busy) the Your apps cards say their room by Account ("Claude Max 20x · not measured yet", "p…@example.com · …"), and Runway by account shows each Account in its own colour (Claude Max 20x violet, p…@example.com olive), the only place Account colours appear.

0.4.1

The maintainer found Home's Your apps cards out of line: badges and buttons at different heights, account lines wrapping to two lines, bars at different heights. Every card now has the same rows: the name line, one profile line, the account line and its bar, then one bottom row with the action on the left and "1 needs you" on the right. An account line is one line, cut, with the whole words in its tooltip; a card with nothing to say there keeps the row empty so the bars and buttons still line up, and when no card in view has room to show (home-typical, home-busy, where nothing is measured yet, as design review 83 asks) the row is left out, with no empty gap. The Open buttons now read "Open" (the card names the app; the screen reader hears "Open Claude app · Work Max"), so ChatGPT's Open sits level with the others and the "1 needs you" chips sit level at the right of the same row (home-typical, home-busy). A profile line no longer wraps: in home-busy "Work M… · 10 sessions" stays one line with the whole name in the tooltip; on a narrower card (1024 wide, checked in the app's preview) the count drops to a bare number beside the profile, as in the rail ("Work Max · 2", "2 sessions" in its tooltip), so the profile keeps its name. The new home-cards capture shows measured room on the three Claude cards ("p…@example.com · 18% left" in amber with a short amber bar, "Claude Max 20x · 70% left" with violet bars at one height), ChatGPT with an empty row in the same place, the kept-alive mark beside the app's name as a small ↻ chip (its words on a wide card, its tooltip "Kept alive: AI Juggler starts it again if it stops" on any), so it never cuts the name or squeezes the profile, and the two closed apps (Lab, Old) after them with their state where an account line goes and Start in the bottom row. Both themes read alike; the amber low state and the violet bars hold on the dark cards. home-empty has no cards; it changed only in the top bar, now led by the ‹ › Back and Forward buttons, which every Home capture shares.

Weak

  • home-busy: in Needs you the fix/limits row's Open its page sits further right than the rows with a ↗ icon, so the buttons in the list don't align.
  • home-empty: Runway by account sits alone in the right column, leaving a large empty area to the left under "Start your first session".
  • rail-8, rail-40: group headers shorten the account to "Pers…" / "Work…" beside the ⚠ count, so two Claude groups are told apart only by their colour dot.
  • view-dark, view-light: the Show lines (Inactive, Quiet ones, Ended today…) have no tick or on/off mark, so the menu doesn't show what is shown.
  • home-typical (0.4): the two Claude app cards (Personal, Work Max) wear two pink-family dots; with the status hues kept clear the App colours hold three families (blue, green, pink), so a fourth App repeats one.
  • home-cards (0.4.1): the closed Lab and Old cards sit on their own row, a little taller than they need beside nothing; with an empty account row on ChatGPT, the first row carries some air when only some Accounts are measured.
  • home-busy (0.4.1): the Work Max profile is cut to "Work M…" beside "10 sessions" at four cards a row; the tooltip has it whole, but two Claude app cards are told apart only by their dots there.
  • home-cards (0.4.1): the ↻ kept-alive chip alone is less plain than the words; it needs its tooltip to be read the first time.

0.4.1 (E21, 2026-11-07): re-shot

What changed, each looked at: Home and the rail as they are now (D-62 App names, the Out/Low marks, kept-alive tiles); the View's Only has App, Account and Project here — Pool joins once you have a pool of your own (this fixture has none; rail-only-pool.test.tsx shows it). Weak, not this family's: the rail cuts App headers to "Work M… Claude Co…" at its width (the 0.4.1 session is fixing it).

94-service-accounts

94-service-accounts: accounts that run through another service (OpenCode Go, Kimi), a missing key, and adding one

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design fixes.

0.3.2

accounts-key-missing shows the 0.3.2 Accounts page well: the "Go and Go Codex" (OpenCode Go · Go Plus · estimate) and Kimi cards stand a step off the dark canvas and as white cards on grey in light, the "Estimate" and "Unknown" pills say up front how far each number can be trusted, and the missing Kimi key is said three ways (the head line "Its Kimi Code API key isn't in this Mac's Keychain", "Add its key…" on the right, and the amber "Its key isn't here" on the profile row). accounts-service is now re-rendered on the 0.3.2 screen: the same two cards with a healthy Kimi ("Kimi · Pro · usage unknown", Replace key…), the 0.3 top bar and rail, and shortened profile folders (~/…/go, ~/…/go-codex, ~/…/kimi) in place of the long Application Support paths, so it now matches its sibling. Add an account is the one solid violet button on both Accounts captures. In add-account-more both Claude Code → and Codex → are solid violet, so the dialog has two equal primaries while the OpenCode Go → and Kimi → rows below keep outline buttons; the installed tools rightly lead, but nothing picks one of the two. service-sheet reads cleanly top to bottom (plan, key with its Keychain sentence, a block per tool with model and profile name, the Estimate note, then Cancel and a solid Add account); the focused Codex model field still shows the placeholder-like value "a-model". The design fixes show in dark as the lighter accent on the logo mark, Add an account, New session, the Estimate pill, the "See it on … site →" links, and in service-sheet the two ticked checkboxes and the Codex field's focus ring; the light captures changed only in the top bar, which stays one row with the whole "7 to clean up" pill. Claude Code and Codex share the same circled-C mark, so on the Go and Go Codex rows and in service-sheet's "Use it with" blocks the mark doesn't tell the two tools apart; only the words do.

Weak

  • add-account-more: Claude Code → and Codex → are both solid violet, two primaries side by side in one dialog.
  • service-sheet: the Codex model field holds "a-model", which reads as a placeholder rather than a real model name.
  • accounts-service, accounts-key-missing, service-sheet: the Codex rows and block carry the same circled C as Claude Code ("© Go Codex", "© Codex"), so the tool mark doesn't distinguish them.

94-usage

94-usage: usage on the Accounts page, a profile's page, a project's Overview and Usage tabs, and the Usage page

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

Five screens (accounts, profile, project-overview, project-usage, usage) at 1280, 1680 and 2000 wide in both themes, each as the first screen and as the whole page (-full), plus accounts and usage at 200 % zoom. In dark every card (stat tiles, Usage over time, By account, the four runway cards, When you work, Top projects, Top sessions, Monthly caps) sits a clear step off the near-black canvas, and in light the white cards on grey keep the stacked bars, rings and heat map crisp; the big numbers (936M, ~$7,369, 460, 230 h) lead, with their "vs the 30 days before" lines and dim labels clearly below. New session is the one solid violet button, and Add an account / New session here stay the obvious entry points. The design fixes are most visible on the dark charts: the stat-tile sparklines, the runway rings (62 %, 59 %, 78 %, 88 %), the When you work heat map, the Sessions bar chart and the Opus share of By model now use the lighter violet, which separates the shades of the heat map better and lifts the bars off the near-black. The light captures changed only in the top bar, which stays one row with "This Mac · Plenty of room" and the whole "7 to clean up" pill, and on the accounts -full captures the Personal profile's folder now reads with a neutral fixture name (~/…/studio-main). At 200 % zoom (accounts-zoom200, usage-zoom200) the rail folds to icons and the cleanup pill is now a broom and "7", but the top bar still sits on two rows (the nav, then the pill, New session, bell and avatar); everything stays readable. The two-column card grid still leaves holes when neighbours differ in height: under Codex Main on accounts-1680x1050 and -2000x1100, beside the tall Top sessions on profile and project-usage at 1680 (the Sessions card stretches with a large empty bottom), and to the right of the lone Agents card on project-overview, with Monthly caps alone at the foot of usage -full. The second pass changes only the rail on accounts, profile and usage (every size, both themes). After its last fix every Open now row keeps its time whole and a short project sits right against its dot, with no gap: "site · viewed 2 min ago", "api · viewed 2 min ago", "web… · viewed 2 min ago".

Weak

  • accounts-1680x1050, accounts-2000x1100: a large empty block under the short Codex Main card beside the tall Work card.
  • profile-1680x1050-full, project-usage-1680x1050-full: the Sessions card stretches to the height of Top sessions, leaving most of it empty.
  • project-overview-*-full: the Agents card stands alone in its row with an empty column beside it.
  • accounts (Personal card): "Not read since its reset · Last read 40% used, 13 h ago; it has reset since" wraps so that "since" sits alone on its own line.
  • accounts-zoom200, usage-zoom200: the top bar still takes two rows at 200 % even with the broom pill; and on accounts-zoom200 "This week 59% left · resets Mon 10:40" leaves "AM" alone on the next line.

95-app-icons

95-app-icons: installed apps' own icons on Home, in the rail, in History and in the Go to session palette

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

The app marks do their job at every size: Claude's and Terminal's own icons lead the Your apps cards (home-1280, home-2000), the rail's group headers (rail-1280, rail-2000), the History rows (history-1280, history-2000) and the Go to session palette's rows (palette-1280, palette-2000), with the profile's colour dot beside the name rather than on the icon. ChatGPT has no icon in this fixture and falls back to a neutral lettered "C" tile, which sits quietly beside the real icons and does not look broken. The app cards and the Needs you and Runway cards stand off the dark canvas and read as white cards on grey in light; Open its page is the solid violet primary, and the amber "1 needs you" chips and "Asks you" labels stay legible in both themes. In dark the lighter accent shows on the logo mark, the header's New session, the rail's New session label and History's focused search ring; the top bar stays one row with "This Mac · Plenty of room" and "7 to clean up" whole at 1280 and 2000. The palettes are re-rendered now on the 0.3.2 palette: "Go to a session or screen, or type what to do…", an Actions group (New session… ⌘N, lit) above Sessions, an icon and "Work Max · in Claude" on every row, "open" on the right and "↩ open its page · ⌘↩ go to its app" in the footer; the last row ("Fix the flaky login test") is cut by the list's bottom edge, which reads as "scroll for more". As elsewhere on Home, the ChatGPT card, the only one without a "1 needs you" chip, drops Open ChatGPT to the chip's line, so it sits lower than the other cards' Open buttons (home-1280, home-2000). After the second pass the rail keeps every time whole and cuts the project instead ("trip-not… · viewed 40 min ago", "ai-ju… · viewed 2 min ago", "payments-… · 4 min ago", "ai-… · 2 min ago") in rail-1280, rail-2000 and every Home and History capture; the palettes changed only in the blurred rail behind them. A later fix closed the gap after short projects and the "2 min ago" / "Probably asks you" collision while keeping every time whole ("blog · 12 min ago", "ai… · 2 min ago", the project giving way). rail-1280-dark now opens with a Needs you group above Open now (Migrate the billing webh…, Answer the release ques…, Tidy the blog's images, each with its state and an open-in-app mark), whose meta lines are cut ("payments-api · Claude · Wo…"); Open now and its groups move down under it.

0.4.1

Your apps cards line up (the maintainer's report): every card has the same rows and one bottom row, the action ("Open"; the card names the app, the screen reader hears it whole) on the left and "1 needs you" on the right. In home-1280 and home-2000 the four Open buttons sit on one line, ChatGPT's included, and the three amber chips sit level at the right of that line. Nothing is measured in this fixture, so the account row is left out rather than kept empty (design review 83 says unknown room says nothing); 94-places/home-cards shows the rows with room measured.

  • rail-1280-dark: the Needs you rows cut their meta ("payments-api · Claude · Wo…", "ai-juggler · Terminal · Work …") and titles ("Migrate the billing webh…").
  • history-2000-dark: the two rows sit at the top of a wide centred list with the rest of the page empty; fine for two rows, thin for a real history.

96-switch-setup

96-switch-setup: what the other account is missing before you switch, and how to set it up

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at the 0.3.2 re-render from its captures as they are now; updated after the 0.3.2 design passes.

0.3.2

These are close crops of the switch's set-up section: can-set-up lists release-notes (ticked, set up as it is) and github (needs your GITHUB_TOKEN) with numbered Terminal steps, a command block and Copy / Check again; turn-on adds computer-use with Show me how, Open claude.ai settings and its own steps; unused says Personal already has everything, with the extra items folded under one disclosure; nothing-missing is now re-rendered as the "Ready to switch to Personal" summary: a "Continues exactly · Beta · Not proven yet" chip on one line, three bullets (the whole conversation carried over, the original stays, nothing needs you), then an open Details with "Goes along" chips (The whole conversation, Project rules, Your uncommitted changes, Subagents' work) and "Recreated on Personal" (The task list). The words are good: each item says what it is, why it is listed ("Used here") and what you do, and the secret is never copied. The command block is a lighter surface that stands off the darker page and a grey well on white in light; in dark the ticked release-notes checkbox shows the lighter accent. The design fix landed where it mattered most: turn-on's "Switch now" is now the solid violet primary in both themes, beside a quiet Cancel. After the second pass the command block wraps at spaces, so "--scope user", "user github" and "command" are no longer split mid-word; it still breaks at hyphens, though: "github --" / "scope user" and "server-" / "github"]", so the flag and the package name still look split even though Copy pastes the command whole (the "-y" stays whole). turn-on's dialog is still wider than the frame: its × sits on the right edge, the computer-use steps box and Switch now are cut at it, and it now opens scrolled one line further, to "Tick it to set github up…", with its heading, the first items and the "…never copies a secret." line above the fold. nothing-missing says "Personal has everything this account has set up" in its bullets and again as "Personal already has 3 of the 3 things this account has set up" at the bottom, and its text runs flush to the frame's left edge.

Weak

  • can-set-up-dark, can-set-up-light, turn-on-dark, turn-on-light: the command block still breaks at hyphens ("github --" / "scope user", "server-" / "github"); mid-word breaks are fixed.
  • turn-on-dark, turn-on-light: the dialog overflows the frame on the right (×, the computer-use steps box and Switch now cut at the edge) and opens scrolled past its heading, release-notes and github's first lines, to "Tick it to set github up…".
  • nothing-missing-dark, nothing-missing-light: the content has no left margin (text flush to the frame's edge); "Personal has everything…" is said twice, in the bullets and in the last line.

0.3.5

The maintainer's report: the last step said "Continue here in AI Juggler", there was no choice between the Claude app and a terminal, and the wizard showed so much that it overwhelmed. Re-shot: can-set-up, turn-on, unused, nothing-missing; new: where-target-step, where-carried-step, where-desktop, where-carry-open, where-no-desktop, where-done-desktop, where-narrow (both themes).

  • **where-desktop:** under "Ready to switch to Personal" and the fidelity badge, "Continue in" offers two cards side by side. **Desktop** has the app's mark, "Claude app · Personal" and a green "Same as the original" tag (the source ran in the Claude app), selected. **CLI** has a terminal mark and "Claude Code in a terminal". Under them, one quiet line: "AI Juggler starts it as it switches." Then one chip, "5 go along · 1 to set up" (warning tint, since one item waits on a decision), and one line: "The original stays open in Claude, untouched. It continues in Claude app · Personal." "Before you switch" keeps only the decision: copy Work Max's notes, one line, with "What does this mean?" closed. Details is closed. The button reads "Switch to Desktop".
  • **where-carry-open:** the chip opened. "Goes along" chips are green with an icon each (speech bubble, document, branch, layers), "Recreated on Personal" is accent with a list icon, and "Not on Personal yet" is amber with a pencil.
  • **where-no-desktop:** Desktop stays visible but greyed, with why: "No desktop app profile on Personal yet. Open a session on it in the app once, and Desktop is offered here." CLI is selected and the button reads "Switch to CLI".
  • **where-done-desktop:** "Switched to Personal", a chip "Opened in Claude app · Personal", "The original stays open in Claude, untouched." and one button, "Open its page".
  • **where-narrow:** the cards stack, the chip and lines wrap, and the footer stays put.
  • **where-target-step / where-carried-step:** the step pills are tinted (done green with a check, current accent), and the Target step ends with "Nothing changes until the last step, where you pick Desktop or CLI and switch."
  • **can-set-up / turn-on / unused:** each set-up row is one line: its name, kind, a coloured verdict tag ("Can copy", "Needs a step from you", "You turn it on") and "Used here". Where it came from, and the token steps, wait behind "What does this mean?" and "Show me how".
Weak (0.3.5)
  • The fidelity badge says "Continues exactly" and "Not proven yet" side by side, which can read as a contradiction. It is the register's honest pair, so it stays until the pair is validated.
  • In the preview the Desktop card's mark is a generic app tile. The real app reads the Claude app's own icon at run time.

97-controls

97-controls: the shared controls and the rail's View

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridEvery line is the shared choice line: icon column, label, ✓ column; Only's values sit in the ✓ column with a chevron.
Hierarchy"View" heading, then Show and Only as small section names, never lines you pick.
SpacingThis branch: every View line is one height.
IconsEvery Show and Only line has its icon, in the app's stroke style.
Shared controlsNo system select, radio or checkbox: the View is a menu of choice lines; Settings › General uses the shared radio cards; History's filters are the shared Picker.
Disabled reasonsReset shows only when something changed instead of sitting greyed.
Plain words"Quiet ones", "Background runs", "Other Macs"; hints say what each is.
Type sizesLabels 14 px, hints and section names 12 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light; the grouping switch's chosen segment is faint in light.
1280×800 and 1024×700At 1024 the list is opened first (the folded column's Group and View door).

Good

  • The View is a real menu: icon · label · hint · ✓ on one grid, Only as submenus with ✓ on the chosen value.
  • Settings › General's equal cards and shared radios.

Weak

  • Hints sat on some lines and not others (Inactive had one, Ended today, Other Macs and Archived didn't), so rows had two heights (fixed in this branch).
  • The menu covers the grouping switch beside it while open.
  • History's Picker open wasn't captured (fixed in this branch).
  • Second re-render, seen by eye on picker-open-history: History's rows stopped with an error said "7 h" and "12 h" beside their neighbours' "5 h ago" (fixed, below).
  • settings-general-cards-1024x700: the three order cards squeeze their hints to one or two words a line ("Stays put. / New ones / join at the / end.") and "Reset my order" wraps onto two lines inside its pill.
  • view-open, view-changed-only-open (both widths): the page heading "5 sessions need you" peeks out half-covered above the View menu's top edge.

Fixed

  • Re-render: the grouping switch's segments size by their words (Pool cut "Account" to "Accou…").
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): the View's rows are one height; a hint sits on the label's line, muted, in shorter words, only where the label needs it.
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): History's Picker open is captured (the fixture always had three projects; the state counted the filters before the lazily loaded screen drew, and skipped).
  • Second re-render (seen by eye): a session stopped with an error has ended, so its row says "7 h ago" like every other ended row (History, a project's Sessions); it was timed like a live one ("7 h"). Vitest: "says \"ago\" for every ended session, one stopped with an error too".

97-handover-told

97-handover-told: what the other tool was told

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThe line sits on the done step's text column, under its paragraph.
HierarchyTitle ("Switched to Personal"), what happened, then the one muted line and its link.
SpacingOne line, one link; no card of its own.
IconsNone needed: a line of facts and a text link, as the other done steps.
Shared controlsNot applicable (no choices).
Disabled reasonsNot applicable.
Plain words"Hidden from Codex: 2 API keys · Left out: 12 earlier turns, 1 note".
Type sizesThe line and its link at the step's body size; nothing under 12 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×700Captured as elements (the done block and the graph), so the window size doesn't change them.

Good

  • One line says what was hidden and what was left out, with Open the handover beside it.

Weak

  • The gate captures only the done block, not the whole sheet.
  • graph-edge: the element crop catches the screen's footer, cut to "ed 1s ago · from AI Juggler's projects…" at the left edge; the "What Codex was told" card runs into it with no bottom edge or padding, and no Open the handover link shows below the line.
  • graph-edge: "Claude · Terminal" on the Work Max node is in the monospace face, "Codex · Terminal" on the Personal node in the body face.

Fixed

  • Nothing open from the review.

0.3.2

done-line and done-open read in a clean order: the "Switched to Personal" title, the sentence in the brighter body level, then "Hidden from Codex: 2 API keys · Left out: 12 earlier turns, 1 note" a step quieter, and the violet Open / Hide the handover link last; in done-open the handover's From / To / Carries / Doesn't carry / Withheld table sits on a raised card with dim labels and brighter values. 97-nav

97-nav: the header, Projects, a project's page, Tasks

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThe header's seven tabs, This Mac's line and the actions share one row; Projects is a table on the page's columns; the Tasks board is four even columns (2×2 at 1024).
HierarchyThe lit tab is the page's title (heavier, raised); a page below a screen names itself in its breadcrumb.
SpacingTabs are snug below 1280 so the row fits at 1024 with the list open.
IconsFilter pickers (Project, Pool, Holder) carry their icons; the tabs are words only, as the reference header.
Shared controlsBoard · Plan is the shared Segmented; the filters are the shared Picker.
Disabled reasonsThe plan has no Holder filter (its epics and stories have no holder) rather than a dead one.
Plain words"Waits on your review", "No one yet", "Nothing waiting".
Type sizesTabs 13 px, body 14 px; nothing under 12 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×700One header row at both; the board folds to 2×2 at 1024.

Good

  • The header is the strongest change: every door visible, the lit tab is the page's title, one row at both widths.
  • A project's page: breadcrumb, folder and branch, tabs with counts, Overview's four figures and the Activity chart on one grid.
  • The Tasks board's four even columns keep full titles and the review card's call to action.

Weak

  • Projects rows at 1280 leave a wide empty middle; the list is one line a project.
  • The board's cards are text only (no mark for the holder's tool); "Waits on your review: open it to approve or send back" is long for a card.
  • The plan's EPIC and STORY in capitals beside a pill read busy.
  • tasks-board, tasks-holder-menu, tasks-open-review: the Blocked card "Rotate signing keys" ends its line with a dangling separator ("api-gateway ·") where a holder would go.
  • every 1280 and 1024 capture with the rail open: the feat/read-model row reads "ai-j… · 3 h ago", with a wider gap after the "…" than the other rows' " · ".
  • project-pool-1280x800: the header chip and facts line say "1 asks you"; the four rows are all "Untitled session · Personal", told apart only by state and time, and the times ("2 h ago") are in the monospace face where every other list uses the body face.

Fixed

  • Re-render: a project's Pool tab names members by the session's title (it said "its folder"); its Subagents tab hides the dead toolbar when there are none; a paired Mac's session page lights Fleet; Fleet's tab no longer wraps the bar to two rows.
  • Re-render: a session's tool and pool are named doors on its facts line ("Claude Code · … · Pool"), so the chips no longer cut to "Running in AI J…".
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): History has a key, ⌘⇧Y, in the same letter scheme as ⌘⇧P, ⌘⇧O, ⌘⇧T, ⌘⇧A and ⌘⇧U (⌘⇧H stays Continue on another account); every header tab's tooltip names its key.
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): a session's header has two chips, its state and one Account menu (the profile's badge and name ▾: Account, Profile, Folder and Open the profile's page). "Running in …" or "Last active …" moved onto the facts line after the tool; the separate Where, Profile, Account and Folder chips went. Go to it, the tool and the pool doors are unchanged.
  • Second re-render: the captures were looked at again after the rail's shorter top and the session header's Account chip; the header row stays one row at 1024 with the list folded, the board stays 2×2, and the session header is two chips (Working, Work Max ▾) with Claude Code, Running in AI Juggler, started, CPU and Pool on the facts line. Nothing else to fix here.

In dark the lighter accent shows on those buttons, the logo mark, the search field's focus ring (projects-search-empty), the review card's violet frame and "Waits on your review" line, and the ✓ in the Holder menu; the light captures change only by a pixel in the This Mac line. The ink levels work too: the plan's EPIC and STORY labels and the board's breadcrumbs ("Checkout v2 › Totals") sit dimmer than the titles. The second pass changed two things here: in every capture the rail's "Probably asks you" feat/read-model row keeps its time whole ("ai-j… · 3 h ago" where it read "ai-jugg… · 3 …"), with a slightly wide gap after the "…"; and session-pool-chip (both widths, both themes) has the terminal's black filling its card to the edge, without the lighter frame in dark or the white one in light, so the "Work Max · session" prompt sits inside a black margin. What remains: project-pool still sets its times in the monospace face ("2 h ago"), says "1 asks you", and lists four identical "Untitled session · Personal" rows; on the board the Blocked card's "api-gateway ·" ends in a dangling separator.

97-pool-canvas

97-pool-canvas: the Pools screen and a pool's canvas

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridHeader, canvas and inspector share the page card's edges; nodes sit on the automatic layout's rows (lead on top).
HierarchyPool name, then its facts line; on the canvas the member's name leads, its state second; the inspector uses the app's section titles.
SpacingThe inspector's rows keep the 8 px rhythm; the palette's folded line is the one header in letter-spaced capitals.
IconsThe canvas toolbar (zoom, fit, Tidy up) and the header's Tasks and Messages carry the app's icons.
Shared controlsCanvas · List is the shared Segmented; Agents is the shared Picker.
Disabled reasonsRemoving a member a pool needs says why on the palette while you drag.
Plain words"1 message waiting", "Paused at the hop limit", "Let it continue", "Don't deliver".
Type sizesFixed in this branch: below 100 % the nodes draw at true size (12 px and up); full cards from 100 %.
Both themesChecked dark and light; the waiting glow reads in both.
1280×800 and 1024×7001280 opens on the canvas at fit; under 1100 px a pool now opens in the List view unless you chose one.

Good

  • Nodes, edges and asks read as a diagram; the inspector's sections (This pool, Live) use the app's section titles.
  • The inspector on a member or an edge answers in place (Allow once · Deny, Deliver); the List view shows the same members as rows with who talks to whom.
  • Tasks and Messages take the page's width; the Pools screen's cards have a live mini-map.

Weak

  • At fit the node text was about 9–10 px, under the 12 px floor, and edge labels ("Paused at the hop limit") were tiny (fixed in this branch, below).
  • The palette's "ADD A SESSION" in letter-spaced capitals is the only such header on the page.
  • The new pool's centred hint card floats in a large empty canvas.
  • The badge's panel and the edge's panel are a card inside a card (the ask's own bordered box in the panel); in canvas-badge-open and canvas-edge-badge-open (both widths) the open panel covers Tax rules research's name until it closes, and at 1024 the "Paused" panel also cuts "Spike: payment sandbox" to "Spike: payme".
  • canvas-full (both widths): the ADD A SESSION palette sits over Planner's card, the 1024 zoom bar lies over the bottom row's text, and the side nodes are cut at the board's left edge ("cart totals", "ayment san…").
  • inspect-member (both widths): the selected node's ⋯ button covers the end of "Checkout copy pass".
  • list-1280x800, list-default-1024x700: the members' times ("2 min ago", "just now") are in the monospace face.

Fixed

  • Re-render: Tasks and Messages over the canvas take the page's width (they sat beside the inspector, a column a few words wide); the Live feed's times stay on one line; "View as list" says "the List view".
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): semantic zoom. Below 100 % the nodes keep their places but draw their words at screen size: the name at 13–14 px, the state dot and word at 12 px, the account line where the node has room, and the waiting count as a badge you click to answer its asks in place. What waits on an edge becomes a small badge ("1 waiting", "Paused") that opens Deliver, or Let it continue and Don't deliver. Full cards, with the ask answered on the card, from 100 % up. The switch fades only with reduced motion off; remembered places, the automatic first layout, Tidy up and Fit work in both forms.
  • Polish (0.3, this branch): under 1100 px a pool opens in the List view unless you chose a view for it; the choice is kept per pool.
  • Second re-render (seen by eye): an agent's ask on an edge (answered on its sender's node, so the edge has no label) no longer takes a label's room. It had pushed the other way's "1 waiting" badge (Planner → Fix the cart totals) about 80 px off its line into empty canvas; the badge now sits on its dashed edge. Vitest: "an ask's edge takes no label's room".
  • Second re-render (seen by eye): in a narrow box at fit (1024 px, 56 %, about 130 px wide) a two-line name is 12 px, so "Spike: payment sandbox" reads whole instead of "Spike: payment…"; at 1280 (69 %) names stay 13 px. Vitest: "a narrow box at fit draws a two-line name at 12 px".
  • Edge routing: an edge between two members of one row that aren't neighbours (Fix the cart totals → Review the checkout steps, paused at the hop limit) ran straight behind the member between them. It read as two short edges through Checkout copy pass, and its "Paused" badge, pushed off that node, read as Checkout copy pass → Tax rules research. An edge that would cross a node now bends round it, on the side where its label has room and, where it can, no other edge runs. Its badge sits on its own line. At fit it arcs under Checkout copy pass with "Paused" on the arc; at 100 % it arcs over, so the full chip doesn't cover that node's ask. Vitest: "an edge never runs behind a node".
  • One count: the header said "1 asks you" (only agents' asks), while the Pools card said "4 need you" and the node badges added up to 4. Both now count the same things in one place (asks, held deliveries, agents' questions, tasks waiting on you, and sessions whose state needs you), and the header says "4 need you". Vitest: "the pool's header says what its Pools card says".

0.3.2

The canvas gains the most from the 0.3.2 surfaces: in dark the dotted board, the nodes and the inspector are three distinct steps, so Planner's violet frame and the amber "needs you" frames on Checkout copy pass, Review the checkout steps and Tax rules research read at a glance in canvas and pools-screen; in light the white nodes and Pools cards sit on a grey ground. The design fixes show in dark only: the lighter accent on the header's New session, the logo mark, Planner's frame and the violet edges, the new pool's focused name field, Change what agents may do… and inspect-member's "1 Allow once", the one filled button beside a quiet "2 Deny"; light changes only by a pixel in the This Mac line. The top bar stays one row, with "This Mac · Plenty of room" at 1280 and "7 to clean up" whole at 1024 (the list folded, no This Mac line). New session and New pool are the solid violet primaries, New message leads messages-panel, and the inspector's section labels (THIS POOL, LIVE, WAITING ON YOU) sit dimmer than their values, which makes inspect-pool's long settings column easy to scan. The old overlaps are all still there: in canvas-badge-open and canvas-edge-badge-open the open panel covers Tax rules research's name, and at 1024 the "Paused" panel cuts "Spike: payment sandbox" to "Spike: payme" (canvas-badge-open-1024, whose panel sits higher, leaves Spike whole); in canvas-full the ADD A SESSION palette sits over Planner's card, the 1024 zoom bar lies over the bottom row's text, and side nodes are cut at the board's left edge; in inspect-member the selected node's ⋯ button covers the end of "Checkout copy pass". The List view's times ("2 min ago") are in the monospace face, as before.

0.4.1 re-score (2026-11-07, AJ-E3305)

The whole family was retaken on release/0.4.1 for the 14-line re-score (AJ-E3305, docs/qa/0.4-walks/design.md) and opened on contact sheets, both themes, 1280 and 1024. Shared with every screen since the last shoot: the header's New session is tinted (one filled button per view), the Floor button is a split control with its own menu, This Mac's line is its dot below 1120 px, and the rail's App headers put the profile first.

A pool you made shows its scope as a chip ("Scope every project") and sealed when kept apart (9b6b9c96b). The New pool wizard (new-pool-*) is in the family: the Build Mac template says "in a later release", not "(0.5)" (9d96ccd06). At 1024 a node's name cuts at 45 % zoom (design.md, under 4, item 3).

98-local-cache

98-local-cache: Settings › Advanced › Local cache, and Privacy's line about it

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's order menu, Usage and Processes. The baseline note says what changed on purpose; this says whether it is good. New family (ADR-0023, D-58).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridOne settings card: the switch row, Size limit, Clear cache, then What's in it as a fold; values and controls on the card's right edge.
HierarchySection title "Local cache", then each row's name over its one-line explanation; the footnote under the card says where it lives.
SpacingRows on the settings cards' rhythm; What's in it's rows carry name, size · items on the right, one line of what it speeds up.
IconsClear cache… carries the broom; the warning carries the app's warning icon; "Turn it on in Privacy" carries the link-out mark.
Shared controlsThe shared switch, the shared Picker for Size limit (a hint under Automatic only), the shared sheet for Clear the cache?.
Disabled reasonsOff: "Turn the cache on to set its limit", "Nothing to clear: the cache is empty" beside greyed controls.
Plain words"Using 47 MB of 1.3 GB. Subagent lists and app icons open without re-reading files."; "Nothing of yours is lost."
Type sizesRow names 14 px, explanations and hints 13 and 12 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light; the low-disk warning reads in both.
1280×800 and 1024×700Both; the menu and the sheet fit at 1024.

Good

  • Every row says what it does in this user's numbers (47 MB, 1.3 GB, 64 MB on a full disk) and what clearing costs, item by item, in the sheet.
  • The search index's row says it has its own switch in Privacy and links there, instead of a second switch here.
  • Privacy's "What it keeps to be quick" line names everything the cache holds and links to Local cache.

Weak

  • The card above (Data: backup & restore) says its purpose twice, inside and under the card.
  • limit-menu (all four): Automatic's hint breaks inside "64 MB" ("between 64" / "MB and 2 GB"); the number and its unit should stay on one line.
  • limit-menu (all four): the open menu covers the end of the footnote under the card ("…never sent. Everythin" at 1280, "…and never sent." at 1024), more than before now that the menu is wider.
  • off-1280x800-light, off-1024x700-light: the off toggle's pale grey track on the white card is barely visible; the switch is the faintest control on the page.
  • The 1024 captures open scrolled, so the backup card's first line is cut under the top bar.

Fixed

  • Second re-render (seen by eye): an empty cache said "Using 0 B of 1.3 GB. Search index opens without re-reading files." (a machine's "0 B", and a claim that something is faster while nothing is kept). It now says "Empty for now. It fills as you use AI Juggler, up to 1.3 GB." Vitest: "summarises off and on in one line" and the clear test.
  • The search index's line read "Search the words of every session answers at once.", a broken sentence. It is now "A search of every session's words answers at once." (the Rust registry, the preview and this gallery).
  • Size limit's menu said "Trims the least recently used first" under each of 250 MB, 1 GB and 4 GB. Only Automatic has a hint now; once a size is picked, its row says "The least recently used goes first."
  • The low-disk capture warned "Your disk is nearly full" under a header saying "This Mac · Plenty of room". The gallery seeded only the cache. A real disk that full (under 5 GB) also makes This Mac say "Under pressure", so the capture now seeds the machine with the same short disk, and the two agree.

Captures

CaptureWhat is on it
on-1280x800, -1024x700The cache on: "Using 47 MB of 1.3 GB…", Size limit Automatic (1.3 GB now), Clear cache (frees 47 MB), What's in it folded.
contents-1280x800, -1024x700What's in it open: Subagent lists 45 MB · 126 items, App icons 2 MB · 38 items, Search index Off with "Turn it on in Privacy".
search-empty-1280x800, -1024x700Search on but empty: "Empty for now. It fills as you use AI Juggler, up to 1.3 GB.", Clear cache greyed, the index's row Empty with "Change it in Privacy".
limit-menu-1280x800, -1024x700Size limit's menu: Automatic ✓ (2 % of free space, between 64 MB and 2 GB), 250 MB, 1 GB, 4 GB.
clear-sheet-1280x800, -1024x700Clear the cache?: frees 47 MB, nothing of yours is lost, what is slower until it fills again; Cancel · Clear cache.
off-1280x800, -1024x700The cache off: "Off: nothing is kept…", the limit and Clear cache greyed with their reasons.
low-disk-1280x800, -1024x700A nearly full disk: 12 MB of 64 MB and the warning "Your disk is nearly full, so the cache keeps to 64 MB and adds nothing until there's room."
privacyPrivacy › What AI Juggler reads: To know what's open, For Last messages, What it keeps to be quick (with Local cache… as its door).

0.3.2

The Local cache card stands clearly off the page in both themes (a lighter card on the darker canvas, a white card on grey), and its rows read in three levels: the row titles, the brighter sentence beneath ("Using 47 MB of 1.3 GB…"), and the quieter figures on the right ("45 MB · 126 items", "Off", "Empty"). The clear-sheet's "Clear cache" is one solid violet next to a plain Cancel, so the dialog's one real action is obvious over the blurred page. low-disk is the strongest capture: the amber warning sits inside the toggle's row, and the header's "This Mac · Under pressure" with its red dot and the rail's "Your Mac is under pressure" agree with it. In off and search-empty the disabled Size limit and Clear cache… are faint, which reads as unavailable, though in light the off toggle's pale grey track on a white card is still the lightest thing on the page. The top bar is one row in every capture (the 1024 ones drop the This Mac line and keep "7 to clean up" whole).

The design fixes show in the dark captures: the lighter violet is on the logo mark, the cache toggle, Automatic's check in limit-menu, "Turn it on in Privacy" and "Change it in Privacy", the Desktop apps and Switching icons and the rail's New session; the light captures moved only by sub-pixel text in the top bar. Size limit's menu is now wider, so Automatic's hint runs on two longer lines, but it still splits "64 MB" across the break ("between 64 / MB and 2 GB"), and the wider menu covers more of the card's footnote while open ("…and never sent. Everythin" at 1280, "…and never sent." at 1024). The 1024 captures still open scrolled so the backup card's first line is cut at the top, and the rail's Needs you line is still cut ("Claude Code's own sign-in, in a t…"). In the second pass the 1280 rail's "Refactor the settings screen" row keeps its age whole ("ai-ju… · viewed 2 min ago" where it read "ai-juggler · viewed 2 mi…"), also blurred behind clear-sheet; nothing else changed.

99-account-page

99-account-page: an Account's own page, and the Accounts screen that leads to it (0.4, D-62, AJ-E3205)

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at 0.4 from its captures; a second section for AJ-E3202 req. 4 (plan, renewal and fee kept on the Account).

0.4

The audit: an account (a subscription) had no page; its name was its folders' names ("Work, Work 2 and 1 more"), every card carried a lone "C" that told Claude Code from Codex for no one, and limits, renewal and fee were set per folder. Four screens, both themes, on the places desktop: the Work Max folder signed in to a Max 20x plan, used by Claude Code in a terminal and by the Claude app's Work profile; a Personal folder on another sign-in.

**accounts-screen.** Each card's title is the Account's own name: "Claude Max 20x" (its plan; no team, no name given), "p…@example.com" (no plan either). Under it one line with what the name doesn't already say (the masked email, the renewal, the fee). No tool letter in the header: the colour dot (its colour in the charts) is the only mark. With accounts on one tool the line doesn't name the tool; on two tools it would. The title is a button with "Open Claude Max 20x's page". Under the limits, one row per App (AJ-E3206 req. 2): "Claude Code · Work Max", its folder's CLI App, marked with the app's icon and the profile's dot as the rail marks it (no "WM" initials), with where its sessions are open and its folder; then "Claude app · Work", the desktop App signed in to the same plan, "3 sessions · 1 h ago" and a chevron to its page.

**account-overview.** "Account" over the name, a tile with the account's chart colour, "Claude Max 20x", then "w…@example.com · 2 apps · 5 open", and New session at the right. Tabs: Overview, Apps 2, Sessions 6, Projects 2, Usage, Settings. Limits first (the account is where limits live), then "Its apps, they share these limits": Claude Code · Work Max and Claude app · Work, each with "3 open · 3 in all" and a chevron to the App's page. Then Open now and Last used across both Apps; each row names its App ("in Claude app · Work", "in Terminal") and not the account. Then Projects. At 1024 the cards stack the same, the rows cut with an ellipsis.

**account-apps.** The two Apps in a card, as rows with icon, app, profile dot and name, counts and a chevron; an App with no session yet would sit below, on a tinted surface under "No sessions yet".

**account-settings.** Name ("Claude Max 20x", "The name AI Juggler gives it: its team, else its plan, else its email.", Rename), then "Plan, renewal and fee" for the whole account: Plan (read from the sign-in, "Max 20x"), Renewal date (a date field), Monthly fee (a field and Save), and the footer "Entered by you, for the whole account: AI Juggler doesn't read your billing."

Fixed while critiquing

  • A session that ended in the Claude app said "in Claude Code" (its tool) on the account's page: rows there name the App the rail puts it under.
  • Rows said "in Claude" where every other place says "Claude app": a row's app is its app's name on screen.
  • The Apps tab's rows sat bare on the page; they're in a card like every other list.

Weak

  • The Sessions tab, read a page at a time from the backend, still names an ended desktop session by its tool; the Overview's rows are right. With AJ-E3206.
  • The renewal date field is the browser's own date control (mm/dd/yyyy in this locale), not the app's styled one.
  • The header tile is only a colour; an account has no mark of its own.

0.4, AJ-E3202 req. 4: plan, renewal and fee set once, on the Account

The audit: plan, renewal date and monthly fee were kept per folder, and the Account's Settings wrote the same value into each of its folders. Two folders on one plan could say two renewal dates, and an App's Settings had its own editors for them. Now the three are stored on the Account. Where an Account's folders agree, the value moves there on the next read. Where they disagree, each folder keeps its own until the user picks one, once. Four new captures per theme, on a second fixture: Work Max and Work 2 on one Max 20x plan, set up apart in 0.3. Their plan (max-20x) and fee ($200) agree, and their renewal dates (12 Nov and 3 Dec) don't. The six older captures were re-shot with the standard fixture.

**accounts-choose.** The Claude Max 20x card's line reads "w…@example.com · $200/month". The fee moved to the Account, and the renewal is left out because it isn't chosen yet. In the header, between the line and "2 apps share this account", is a warning chip, "⚠ Choose its renewal date", with the same shape and tone as the app's other warning chips. It's a button that opens the Account's Settings. Under the limits, each App row shows its own date until the choice: "Claude Code · Work Max … Renews in 11 days" and "Claude Code · Work 2 … Renews 3 Dec", so nothing is hidden. Light: the chip is the warning-soft fill with dark amber text and passes axe contrast. (A first version with a hand-made 10% amber fill failed contrast in light and was swapped for the Chip primitive.)

**account-choice.** The Account page opens on Settings with the same chip beside the name. The header facts are "w…@example.com · $200/month · 3 apps · 5 open". Under Name, "Plan, renewal and fee" starts with the choice as the first row of its card, on a faint amber tint:

  • The question, after a warning icon: "Work Max and Work 2 say different renewal dates: 12 Nov or 3 Dec?" It uses the maintainer's own wording, with the Apps' profile names and short dates.
  • One line on what the choice changes: "An account has one renewal date: renewal reminders and its card use it, for every app on it. Until you choose, each app keeps its own."
  • Two option buttons, each with two lines: the value in body type ("12 Nov", "3 Dec") over the App that says it in muted type ("in Claude Code · Work Max", "in Claude Code · Work 2").

Then the three rows:

  • Plan: "Max 20x", said as words and not as the stored "max-20x". Save stays off until the text changes. Plans are compared by their words, so "Max 20x" and "max-20x" never ask.
  • Renewal date: an empty date field, described as "Its apps say different renewal dates: choose above, or set one here."
  • Monthly fee: 200 and Save.

The footer reads "Entered by you, once for the whole account and every app on it: AI Juggler doesn't read your billing."

**account-chosen.** After a click on 3 Dec, the choice row and the header chip are gone. The facts now read "Renews 3 Dec · $200/month". The date field holds 12/03/2026 and its description is back to "When the plan renews: reminders come before it." Nothing asks again. Each folder's own date stays stored, but the folders aren't written any more (the Rust tests pin this).

**app-settings-terms.** The App page for Claude Code · Work Max, Settings tab. Under Name, a new "Plan, renewal and fee" section built from the same rows as every Settings pane:

  • A first row, "On Claude Max 20x" / "They're its account's, set once for every app on it.", with **Set on its account** at the right. It opens the Account's Settings tab (the test clicks it).
  • Read-only rows below: Plan "Max 20x", Renewal date "3 Dec", Monthly fee "$200/month". The values are right-aligned in ink.

The old "Plan and renewal" cell, with its date field, Clear and "Flat fee $/mo" editor, is gone from the App's Settings card. That card now starts with Tidying up. An App whose sign-in isn't read yet has no Account and keeps those editors (vitest pins this).

**accounts-screen, account-overview(-1024), account-apps, account-settings (re-shot).** The standard fixture with nothing to choose. account-settings now has the editable Plan row ("Max 20x", Save) where it used to say "as its sign-in says", which was wrong: a plan is entered, never read. It also has the empty fee field and the new footer. The re-shoot also picks up two earlier 0.4 changes that this family hadn't been shot with: the Claude app profile is now named by its Claude Code folder ("Claude app · Work Max", AJ-E3203), and the Account's dot, its chart line and both App dots share one colour (AJ-E3206's one mark per App). Before, the card was orange and the Claude app's dot blue.

Fixed while critiquing
  • The choice chip was a hand-made amber pill that failed axe contrast in light. It's the Chip primitive now, wrapped in a button, and both the card and the page use one ChooseChip.
  • The Plan field showed the stored "max-20x" under a description that said "Max 20x". It shows the words now, and the description no longer repeats them.
  • The fee read "$200 a month" in the choice and the App rows but "$200/month" on the cards and the header. It's "/month" everywhere.
Weak
  • "2 apps share this account" on the card counts folders, while the card lists three App rows (with the Claude app). That's AJ-E3206's count, not this task's.
  • The choice offers only the values its Apps said. Another date is set in the row below it ("or set one here"), which is one more step than a third option would be.
  • The renewal field is still the browser's own date control (mm/dd/yyyy).

0.4: a renewal said as a date

The weak point above: the Account page's header and the Accounts card's App rows said a renewal as the stored ISO string ("Renews 2026-12-03"), while the choice and the App's Settings said "3 Dec". Every renewal label now comes from one rule (renewalLabel), and its date is said by the same words the choice uses (dateWords: "3 Dec", with the year only when it isn't this one). The day before, it says "Renews tomorrow" (it said "Renews in 1 days"). Four captures changed; the other fourteen are as they were.

**accounts-choose (both themes).** The Work 2 row under the Claude Max 20x card reads "Claude Code · Renews 3 Dec · ~/.claude-claude-work-2" (it read "Renews 2026-12-03"). Work Max's row still says "Renews in 11 days": inside two weeks a renewal counts down, as before.

**account-chosen (both themes).** After 3 Dec is chosen, the header facts read "w…@example.com · Renews 3 Dec · $200/month · 3 apps · 5 open". The header, the card's rows, the choice ("12 Nov or 3 Dec?") and the App's Settings ("3 Dec") now say a date one way. The row is no wider than before: "Renews 3 Dec" is shorter than the ISO string it replaces.

Weak
  • The renewal date field itself is still the browser's control and shows 12/03/2026 beside "Renews 3 Dec" in the header: the same day, written two ways on one screen, until the field is the app's own.

0.4, AJ-E3206 req. 2: one mark and one colour per App

Re-shot after each App and each Account got one stored colour. All eighteen captures changed.

**account-overview, account-overview-1024, account-apps (both themes).** Before, the round mark by "Claude Max 20x", the dot by Claude Code · Work Max and the dot by Claude app · Work Max were one blue, because all three were coloured by the name "Work Max". Now the Account's round mark is rose, Claude Code · Work Max's dot is magenta and Claude app · Work Max's is orange: two Apps, two colours, and an Account in a third colour and a different shape. The Limits line is the Account's rose. The rail's dots and row edges match the Apps' rows here.

**account-settings, account-choice, account-chosen (both themes).** The header's round mark is rose. The rail on the left shows each App's own colour on its header, rows and edges.

**accounts-screen, accounts-choose (both themes).** Each Account card's dot is its own: Claude Max 20x rose, the personal account teal (before: blue and violet, its first folder's colours). The card's limits line is the same colour. The App rows below it carry their App's dot: Claude Code · Work Max magenta on its icon's corner, Claude Code · Work 2 orange, Claude app · Work Max orange or rose depending on how many folders the capture has.

Weak
  • In accounts-choose, with three folders, Claude app · Work Max takes the slot whose hue is the Account's rose. With eight hues, the fifth App always shares a hue with the first Account, so App and Account are told apart by shape: a dot on the icon's corner against the round mark by the Account's name.
  • The personal account's teal is 27° from the live colour. It sits by the Account's name, never by a session's state.

0.4, AJ-E3103: the one related list and the one row

Recaptured: account-overview and account-overview-1024, in both themes. The rest were left as they were.

**account-overview.** The Open now and Last used cards use the one row. Line 2 reads "◆ ai-juggler · ● Claude Code · Work Max · just now", ending in the state word. The row names its App with the App's colour dot, as the rail does. The Account is the page's own and gets no colour there, following the 2026-11-03 ruling. In a half-width card the App's name truncates before the time does.

Weak
  • In a half-width card the App's name is cut to "Claude Code · …" or "Claude…". The icon says which app it is, but not which profile.

0.4.1 re-score (2026-11-07, AJ-E3305)

The whole family was retaken on release/0.4.1 for the 14-line re-score (AJ-E3305, docs/qa/0.4-walks/design.md) and opened on contact sheets, both themes, 1280 and 1024. Shared with every screen since the last shoot: the header's New session is tinted (one filled button per view), the Floor button is a split control with its own menu, This Mac's line is its dot below 1120 px, and the rail's App headers put the profile first.

The Account page's line names it without its e-mail ("2 apps · 5 open"; 059ff2db9), the window says its name, and with nothing measured its Limits end in "Measure its limits ›" where measuring can be switched on (d8dd6b4a1). The Accounts cards' link reads "Measure its limits ›" and opens the Account. Renewal: the newer date chosen, ⌘↩ (account-choice).

99-account-quality: how an Account's sessions went

Design critique (D-57) against the Account page (99-account-page) and a session's signals (92-session-pages). New family (AJ-E2903).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridA section under Limits; each signal a row: its name in a left column, the figure and its source on the right.
HierarchyThe figure bold, its source muted under it; a figure that isn't reported is plain, not bold.
SpacingThe section's row rhythm; the pending note under the rows.
IconsThe gauge icon Limits uses.
Shared controlsSection.
Disabled reasons"Codex doesn't record errors in its files", "Not reported by Codex" in place of numbers.
Plain words"9 errors or retries / 300 turns" (never a bare percentage), "2 times probably stuck", "1 session just ended: its figures come in a moment."
Type sizes14 px figures, 12 px sources.
Both themesChecked.
1280×800 and 1024×700Both; the two columns stack below 560 px of content.

Good

  • Each figure says where it comes from, as a session's signals do, and there's no overall score.
  • A tool without the data says so in its own name rather than showing zeros.

Weak

  • "Not once" for stuck episodes is plain where a reader might want reassurance; it's honest but quiet.
  • The section shows no comparison with the account's own earlier weeks.

Fixed

  • Nothing on first render.

0.4.1 re-score (2026-11-07, AJ-E3305)

The whole family was retaken on release/0.4.1 for the 14-line re-score (AJ-E3305, docs/qa/0.4-walks/design.md) and opened on contact sheets, both themes, 1280 and 1024. Shared with every screen since the last shoot: the header's New session is tinted (one filled button per view), the Floor button is a split control with its own menu, This Mac's line is its dot below 1120 px, and the rail's App headers put the profile first.

Unchanged in design: the measured week ("9 in 300 turns", "2 times", "71% on average") and Codex's "doesn't record errors or context" line. Its figures still don't open their sessions (design.md, under 4, item 1).

99-app-page

99-app-page: an App's own page (0.4, D-62, AJ-E3204)

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at 0.4 from its captures.

0.4

The audit's G1/G2: clicking "Claude app · b-work" in the rail opened a folder's page titled "Account" that also listed Terminal sessions; nothing answered "this app: its sessions, projects, settings". Five screens, both themes, on the places desktop (two Claude app profiles and ChatGPT running, a Lab profile kept alive that's down), each opened the way a user would: from the rail's App header, or from a Home tile's app name.

**app-overview.** "App" over the name, the Claude app icon with Work's profile dot beside it (D-38), and "Claude app · Work" in the page title size. One facts line: "Claude app · Running · kept alive · 3 sessions · 2 open". One primary button at the right, "Go to it". Tabs with counts: Overview, Sessions 3, Projects 2, Usage, Processes (it has open sessions), Settings. Two cards side by side: "Open now 2 open" with its two sessions as the shared session rows, and "Last used" with the one that ended. Below, its account's Limits card (the folder's runway and the last 7 days of tokens), then "Projects, where it works, most recent first", each with its session count and when. The rows don't repeat "in Claude · Work Max" or the account: the page says both once. At 1024 the two cards stay side by side and the rows cut their second line with an ellipsis.

**app-sessions.** "3 sessions · 2 open" then the paged list, open first: title, state and branch, the project column, when. No account column (the same on every row here).

**app-settings.** Two sections drawn as Settings › Desktop apps draws them: Name ("Claude app · Work", "The name AI Juggler gives it: its app, then its profile.", Rename), and Start and keep alive (the same row as Settings: Open, and the Keep alive switch on).

**app-closed.** Lab, down and kept alive: "Not running · keep alive starts it · No sessions yet", the primary button is "Start", and the body is one card, "No sessions yet", saying what will show there and what to do first, instead of three empty cards. No Usage or Processes tab: nothing to show.

**app-cli.** "Claude Code · Work Max", "Claude Code in a terminal · 3 sessions · 3 open", primary "New session". Its background import run is under Open now (it's running), so "Last used" says "Everything here is open now."; its folder's Limits and its one project follow.

Fixed while critiquing

  • The Settings rows were list items outside a list (axe: listitem); they're rows of a section now, with the Settings card around them.
  • Every row said "in Claude · Work Max" and the account, and an ended session said "in Claude Code" on the Claude app's page; rows on an App's page leave both out.
  • A running background session sat under "Last used"; it's under Open now.
  • An App with no sessions showed three empty cards ("No other sessions here yet."; "its sessions so far ran outside one"); now one card says what to do.
  • Settings › Desktop apps named a profile "Claude · Work Max" (its account) while the rail said "Claude app · Work"; both say the App's name now.

Weak

  • The facts line doesn't name the Account yet ("on Work Max", a door to the Account page): that comes with AJ-E3205 and AJ-E3206.
  • Limits on a desktop App show its folder's, and say "Not measured yet" where the folder has no reading; whose room it is should be said in the card's title once the Account page exists.
  • Processes lists its open sessions' processes, not the desktop app's own helper processes.
  • Rename is in Settings only; the header's ⋯ menu (rename, hide from the rail, show the folder) is still to come.

0.4, AJ-E3206 req. 2: one mark and one colour per App

Re-shot after each App and each Account got one stored colour. All twelve captures changed. They also catch up with changes made since the last shoot: the Claude app's profile reads "Work Max" (its Code folder's name), the Accounts tab is lit, and a CLI App's header has its ⋯ menu.

**app-overview, app-overview-1024, app-sessions, app-settings (both themes).** Claude app · Work Max's dot beside its name is orange. Before, it was the blue that every "Work Max" thing wore, the CLI App and the Account included. The same orange is on its rail header ("Claude app · W…"), the left edge of its rows, its Needs you rows' corner dots and, in the 1024 captures, the folded rail's icons. Claude Code · Work Max, a different App on the same folder, is magenta in the Needs you row ("Answer the release ques…"). The Limits card's line is the Account's rose now, not the App's colour: the room is the Account's. So the page shows two colours on purpose, the App's dot in the header and its Account's on its limits, and neither is a status colour.

**app-cli (both themes).** Claude Code · Work Max's header dot is magenta, the same as its rail rows and Needs you dot. Its Limits line is the Account's rose. Before, the header was orange and so was the line.

**app-closed (both themes).** Claude app · Lab's dot is green (its stored slot) and matches its tile on Home and its Inactive apps row.

Weak
  • A green App dot right after the app's icon in a header can read as "running" next to "Not running". It's the palette's green, 23° from the success colour, and the page says "Not running" in words.
  • The App's dot and its Account's line are two hues on one page, and nothing on it says which is which. The Account's colour is explained only on the Accounts cards ("This account's colour in the charts").
  • Magenta (Claude Code · Work Max) and rose (its Account) are 30° apart. They're told apart by shape (a small dot against a line and a round avatar), less by hue.

0.4, AJ-E3103: the one related list and the one row

Recaptured: app-sessions, app-overview, app-overview-1024 and app-cli, in both themes. app-closed and app-settings were left as they were.

**app-sessions.** The list opens in sections (Working, Open, Last 7 days). Each row now shows the project's mark and name and when on line 2, with the state word at that line's end. Before, the state led line 2, the project sat in a column and the time in another. With three rows there is no search and there are no chips (they appear past 8).

**app-overview, app-overview-1024, app-cli.** The Open now and Last used cards use the same row. In a card at 1024, the state word stays at the end of line 2 and the place line truncates first.

0.4.1 re-score (2026-11-07, AJ-E3305)

The whole family was retaken on release/0.4.1 for the 14-line re-score (AJ-E3305, docs/qa/0.4-walks/design.md) and opened on contact sheets, both themes, 1280 and 1024. Shared with every screen since the last shoot: the header's New session is tinted (one filled button per view), the Floor button is a split control with its own menu, This Mac's line is its dot below 1120 px, and the rail's App headers put the profile first.

A CLI App's main button reads "New session here" (c61757a87); its Launch settings tab is counted ("Launch settings 2"); whom it shares its room with is said once, in the header, not again under Limits (9d96ccd06). A desktop App: Restart… beside Go to it.

99-app-profiles

99-app-profiles: Claude app profiles found when they aren't running (0.4, D-62, AJ-E3203)

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at 0.4 from its captures.

0.4

The audit's decision 5 and scenario A6: someone with six Claude app profiles saw only the ones running today, so the App list hid what they have. Five screens, both themes, on the places desktop (the Work Max and Personal profiles running, Lab kept alive and down, Old seen 9 days ago), with two more profile folders, Design and Research, beside them in ~/Profiles. Each is reached the way a user would: Work Max's page from the rail's App header, then Home, then Settings › Desktop apps from the menu.

**ask** (1280) and **ask-1024.** Work Max's App page, Overview. One card above "Open now" says what would be read and where: "Find this app's other profiles. Claude app keeps each profile in a folder of its own, in ~/Profiles. AI Juggler can read the names of the folders there, and nothing inside them, so the profiles that aren't running show too." Under it, quieter: "You're asked once. Settings › Desktop apps can turn it off later." Two buttons at its right, "No thanks" (ghost) then "Find them" (secondary): the page's one filled button stays "Go to it". The folder is in the mono face so the path reads as a path. At 1024 the card's text wraps to two lines and the buttons stay beside it. The header reads "Claude app · Work Max": the profile is named by the Claude Code folder its sessions use, as the user names it there, not by its own folder ("Work").

**found.** After "Find them": the card becomes one line, "Found 2 more profiles: Design, Research. Each is an app of its own now, with Start.", with OK. The rail's Inactive apps lists four now: Lab and Old (seen, with when), then Design and Research, "Not running", each with its Start button. They come after the ones used, since they were never seen running.

**found-app.** Design's own page, from its name in the rail: "Claude app · Design", facts "Claude app · Not running · found with your other profiles · No sessions yet", the primary button "Start". One card says what will fill the page. No Usage or Processes tab (nothing to show yet).

**home.** Home's Your apps after "Show 2 more apps": the running apps' tiles, then the closed ones, Lab (kept alive) and Old first, then Design and Research, each "Not running · found with your other profiles" and "Start Claude app".

**settings.** Settings › Desktop apps, Your apps: Design and Research are rows like the others, with Start and Keep alive but no Forget (a found profile would come straight back; its folder or the switch decides). Below the rows, the switch "Find profiles that aren't running": "Reads the names of the folders beside a profile that ran (~/Profiles), and nothing inside them. Off forgets the profiles found that way." It's on after the yes.

Fixed while critiquing

  • The result line ("Found 2 more profiles…") stayed on screen when moving from Work Max's page to Design's: it's now the page's own, and a new page starts without it.
  • The rail said "Not running · just now" for a profile only found by its folder, as if it had just been seen; it says "Not running" and sorts after the apps you've used. Home's closed tiles put them after the used ones too.
  • Settings offered Forget on a found profile, which would come back a minute later; it's gone for those rows.
  • "Asked once." read like a fragment; it says "You're asked once. Settings › Desktop apps can turn it off later."

Weak

  • The ask sits above "Open now", the page's most used card. It's shown once, until answered, and it's the only thing on the page that needs an answer, so it leads; it's still one more card the first time.
  • A found profile has no Account until its sessions run once (nothing inside its folder is read to learn it), so its page shows no Limits, and its name is its folder's ("Design") until then.
  • The Settings switch is a row without an icon below rows with app icons; it governs the list, so it stays in the same section.
  • The rail's open App headers still cut "Claude app · Personal" to "Claude app · P…" at 1280 (from before this change).

99-conversation-feed

99-conversation-feed: a Claude session's conversation, followed live

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814) for 0.3.5 (ADR-0024), against the Claude app's Code tab (docs/research/native-conversation-display.md §1) and the reference screens of 92-session-pages. The captures are the Conversation card alone, at 1280×800, both themes.

What each capture shows

  • **stretches**: a Claude turn drawn as the Claude app draws it: the narration line, then each run of work as one line ("Ran the checkout test 40 times", "Read 2 files, searched code", opened onto its three steps), the texts in full between them. No "Worked for" row.
  • **running**: opened at the bottom; the person's mid-turn message as their bubble, then the turn still working with its current run in the "-ing" form ("Running the unit tests") and a pulsing dot.
  • **new-messages**: the reader scrolled to the top while the reply arrived: the view did not move, and a "New messages ↓" pill sits over the bottom of the list.
  • **banner**: the latest changes couldn't be read: one line under the header says so; the turns stay, and the newest is still in view.

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridTurns on the card's 760 px reading column; fold lines indented with the agent's text under its name.
HierarchyWords in ink at body size; fold lines muted at meta size, so the text reads first, as in the Claude app.
Spacing12 px between a turn's parts, 24 px between turns, unchanged from 0.3.4.
IconsThe fold's chevron is the one the 0.3.4 work row used; the pill's arrow is a plain down arrow.
Shared controlsFold lines are buttons (focus ring, aria-expanded); the pill is a button; no system controls.
Disabled reasons"Show earlier messages" is disabled only while a page loads or the follow restarts.
Plain words"New messages", "Couldn't read the latest changes. Trying again.", "Running the unit tests".
Type sizesText 14 px, fold lines and banner 13 px.
Both themesChecked dark and light; the running dot is the live colour in both.
1280×800 and 1024×7001280×800 only: the card is the same column at 1024 (92-session-pages covers the page at both).

Good

  • A Claude turn reads like the app: the words, with the work between them one quiet line each.
  • New words never move a reader who scrolled up; the pill is the only change on screen.
  • A problem reading is a line over the turns, not an empty card.

Weak

  • The pill covers a line of text while it shows (it floats over the list, as the apps' own do).
  • The banner is neutral grey; "Trying again" problems and "it ended" notes look the same.

SpacingThe rows' rhythm holds; Details opens below the row without moving the columns.
