99-floor-list: the Floor list

Design critique (D-57) against the approved wireframes (D-78) and the History and Usage tables. New family (AJ-E3512).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridOne section per floor; one card per App with a header (initials, name, kind, account and usage at the right) and a table of its sessions. The columns have fixed widths, so they line up from one App to the next.
HierarchyFloor headings, then App names in bold, then sessions; a state that waits on you is bold with a warning-coloured glyph.
Spacing20 px between floors, 12 px between Apps.
IconsEach state's glyph (! ▸ ? ⏸ ✕ ✓ · –) beside its word; the glyph is decoration, the word says it.
Shared controlsButton (ghost, small), the screen header with its action.
Disabled reasonsAn App with no sessions says "No sessions · last active …" and still offers Show on the Floor.
Plain words"2 floors · 5 Apps · 5 waiting on you", "Busy processor · 4 min", "Work Max · 62% left".
Type sizes12 px table text, 11 px column heads, 14 px App names.
Both themesChecked.
1280×800 and 1024×700Both.

Good

  • The same facts as the canvas, in reading order, with "Show on the Floor" as the door back to it.
  • A new wait is said once, politely; sessions AI Juggler runs are said by the main window's announcer, not twice.
  • 150 rows render in 3.3 ms median, 6.4 ms p95 (AJ-E3512 timing.json, re-run 2026-11-06), within 16 ms.

0.4.1 (AJ-E3517)

The list now carries the Floor card's words. Resting on a row (keyboard focus, or the pointer for 250 ms, as on the Floor) reads that one session's question or latest step through the same reader as the card (its setting and redaction) and shows it in a bar at the list's foot: "Checkout copy · Asks: Should I keep the old wording on the checkout button, or use the new one from the design file?" (said-1280x800, said-1024x700, both themes). The bar is a raised white card in light and a raised surface in dark, the session's title in semibold, then the words; it is said politely to a screen reader and goes 300 ms after the row is left. Nothing is read for rows nobody rests on. The list captures (list-*) changed only in the "Waiting on you 5" chip, which now has a space before its count, so its accessible name is its visible words (axe: label-content-name-mismatch fixed).

Weak

  • said-1024x700: the bar floats over the rows at the window's foot and can cover one for the moment it shows (it never takes clicks).
  • "Waiting on you" counts stuck, done and out-of-room sessions along with asks; that is the Floor's own meaning but wider than the word "asks" in the rows.

Fixed

  • axe (0.4.1): the "Waiting on you" chip's name was "Waiting on you: 5" beside its visible "Waiting on you5"; now its words.
  • Columns drifted between App tables (auto widths); now fixed widths.
  • axe: "Show on the Floor" buttons had names that didn't start with their visible words; now "Show on the Floor: <session>".

99-floor-looks: the Floor in Pixel art and in Full resolution (D-80)

Design critique (D-57) of the Floor's two looks, rendered by the golden-frame test (AJ_D80_CAPTURE) at 1280 × 800 on a Retina-scaled frame, the building view and floor 1 in each. The 3D Floor has one look in light and dark alike (its sky and light don't follow the app's theme); the setting that picks the look is in 99-floor-settings, in both themes.

Screenshot notes

  • pixel-art-building-1280x800: the building in Pixel art, both floors; floor 1's App labels stand below floor 2's slab, none over another, each over its own floor; long names whole ("INDUSTRIAL TOOL · HOME X1 14% HOT").
  • full-resolution-building-1280x800: the same view at the frame's own pixels: smooth edges, sharp textures, the same labels in the same places.
  • pixel-art-floor-1-1280x800: floor 1 in Pixel art; every label whole and inside the window ("ANOTHER TOOL · SPARE", "LAST TOOL · SPARE !1 14%" at the right edge), none under a ! or ✓ balloon.
  • full-resolution-floor-1-1280x800: floor 1 at full resolution: the same scene, the same labels, outlines and the hover ring at Pixel art's weight.

Checklist

CheckFinding
LabelsNo two overlap, none under a balloon, the switcher or the hint, all inside the window; a moved one keeps a 1 px stick in its area's colour (the golden test area_chips_never_overlap checks every view at three sizes, a crowded office included).
FloorsIn the building view a label stays over its own floor: it moves down or aside before up and never onto another floor's slab.
Both looksThe same scene, labels, outlines and hover; only the pixels differ.
Plain wordsLabels in the Floor's own capitals; long names end in ".." at 24 characters, never on the "·".
CostMeasured beside each other (docs/qa/floor/budgets-2026-11-05.md and -full.md): within D-79 at the same window; on a large window Full resolution goes past the CPU budget, which the setting says.

Good

  • Full resolution reads as the same office, just finer: nothing moves between looks.
  • Labels now read as belonging to their floor in the building view.

Weak

  • Full resolution's cost grows with the window: on a large or Retina window it's over the Floor's 2% CPU budget while animating.

Fixed

  • Labels printed over each other in the building view and under balloons on floor 1, and two were cut at the right edge; fixed before these captures (the placement, the floor rule, the sticks).

99-inactive-apps

99-inactive-apps: the rail grouped by App is about apps (0.3.4, D-64)

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at 0.3.4 from its captures.

0.3.4

The maintainer's report: a Claude app that quit overnight vanished from the rail with no way to start it, and "23 inactive" listed sessions from all over the place. Four screens, both themes, on the places desktop (two Claude profiles and ChatGPT running, a Lab profile kept alive that's down, an old one), plus a CLI app with nothing open: Claude Code on a "Side project" folder with two sessions that ended yesterday and two days ago.

**rail-inactive-apps.** Under Open now, each app with sessions that aren't open ends with a quiet link in the accent colour, "Last 1 inactive", with a chevron at the right like the section heads'. After the last open app comes a section head, "Inactive apps 3", with a clock icon and the secondary-text colour, so it sits below Open now's green in weight. Its rows are drawn like place headers: the app icon, the app name in secondary text (dimmer than an open app's), the profile badge and name, then a second line, "Not running · 20 min ago" or "20 h ago · 2 sessions". The rows are in most-recently-used order. Each has one 24 px action at the right, in the same outline as an open app's ↗: ▷ Start for a desktop app that's down, + New session for a CLI app. A row with sessions has the fold chevron at the left; one without keeps the space, so the icons line up. "Also:" no longer lists inactive sessions: "1 ended today · 1 background · History ›".

**rail-last-3.** "Last 1 inactive" unfolded under Claude app · Personal shows its ended session as a normal row ("Ended") under the link, its chevron turned down. The Side project app unfolded shows its two sessions with the folder's colour edge, newest first.

**rail-idle-menu.** A right-click on Claude app · Lab opens its menu: the name as the heading, Start, then Keep alive with the violet ✓, as the place header's menu draws it.

**home-closed-apps.** Your apps: the four open tiles, then Lab and Old as dashed tiles with "Start Claude app".

Weak

  • A row's second line can still be cut at the narrowest rail width when the profile name is long. The full words are its accessible name, but there's no tooltip.
  • An inactive app with more than 3 sessions shows only its last 3; the rest are in History until the App page (0.4) lists them.
  • The menus have no icons, matching the place header's menu. Icons for both menus come with 0.4's App page actions.

0.4, AJ-E3206 req. 2: one mark and one colour per App

Re-shot after each App and each Account got one stored colour. All eight captures changed.

**rail-inactive-apps, rail-last-3, rail-idle-menu (both themes).** Each App's dot is now the same in Open now, Inactive apps and Home. Claude app · Work is orange on its header, edge and tile (before, blue). Claude Code · Work Max is magenta (before, orange: the same orange the Account had). Lab is green, Old violet and Side project blue. The Side project's sessions keep its blue edge when unfolded.

**home-closed-apps (both themes).** Your apps' tiles have the rail's colours: Personal pink, Work Max orange, Claude Code · Work Max magenta, and the remembered Lab green and Old violet. "Runway by account" shows each Account's round mark in its own colour, rose and teal. Before, they were orange and violet, the colours of their first folders. The Needs you rows' corner dots are their Apps' colours.

Weak
  • Violet (Old) and magenta (Claude Code · Work Max) are 20° apart. At the rail's 6 px dot they're hard to tell apart, though the name beside each dot is the real label.

99-keep-alive

99-keep-alive: Open starts an app that isn't running, and Keep alive (0.3.3)

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at 0.3.3 from its captures.

0.3.3

Five screens, both themes, on the places desktop (two Claude profiles and ChatGPT running, a Lab profile kept alive that's down, an old one).

**home-kept.** Your apps gains a fifth tile after the four session tiles: Claude with the Lab badge, a dashed border, "Not running · keep alive starts it" in the quiet grey, and Start Claude in the same outline button as the other tiles' Open. The dashed edge says "not here" without a colour of its own, and the tile keeps the row's grid width. The Work Max tile carries a small violet "kept alive" pill under its count. A first pass wrote "· kept alive" inline, which wrapped to a line starting with a dot; the pill wraps as a unit.

**home-paused.** The same tile, its line in the warning colour: "Keep alive paused: it stopped 3 times in 10 minutes", on two lines, Start Claude under it. The tile reads as the one thing in Your apps to look at, which is right for a pause.

**settings-your-apps.** Settings › Desktop apps opens on the new section, its heading framed (the jump's focus) above five rows. Each row has the app's icon, its name with the profile as Home names it ("Claude · Work Max"), a grey state line, then Start or Open, the "Keep alive" word and its switch. Kept-alive rows come first. The paused Lab row adds the amber "Open it to start it and keep it alive again." with the warning icon, as other Settings warnings do. Forget shows only on the idle closed row (Old), as a quiet ghost button. Every switch and button lines up in one right-hand column. The footer explains Start and Keep alive and their limits in three lines.

**session-open-in.** The ended session's header: Continue in AI Juggler (the one primary), then "Open in Claude" and "Copy resume command" as secondaries. The button names the app only; the profile is the Personal chip under the title, and the full "Open in Claude · Personal" is its accessible name and tooltip. A first pass printed the profile in the button and cut the title to "Add pagination to the ord…"; now it reads "…to the orders end…". Where it runs says "App · Not open now · last in Claude" above "Profile · Personal", so the card and the button agree on where the session was.

**rail-menu-keep.** A right-click on Claude · Work Max in the rail opens its menu: Open its page, Bring it forward, Keep alive with a violet ✓ at the right, Fold. The ✓ is drawn as the rail's order menu draws its own. A first pass had the item ticked for screen readers but showed no tick.

0.3.4

Re-taken after D-64 (the rail's App grouping is about apps) and the fix for the shell scrolling under the rail.

**home-kept, home-paused.** Your apps now gives every remembered closed app a tile, not only the kept-alive ones: Lab first (kept alive), then Old ("Not running · seen 9 days ago"). Both use the dashed border and Start. The tiles name the app as the open tiles do, "Claude app" with the profile badge under it, and "Start Claude app". The accessible name is "Start Claude app · Lab", so it still contains the visible words (axe caught the first pass, which said "Start Claude · Lab"). In the rail, the Claude app groups end with a quiet "Last 1 inactive" link and its chevron.

**settings-your-apps.** It opens with the top bar in place again. A first pass of the 0.3.4 rail had shifted the whole window up 76 px: the new inactive-app rows' screen-reader names were positioned by the rail, outside its scrolling list. The rows' buttons now hold them, and so do the place headers', which had the same latent problem far down a long list. The rows still say "Claude · Lab"; Settings' words follow D-62 in 0.4.

**session-open-in, rail-menu-keep.** Unchanged in substance. While a search hides an app's open sessions, that app isn't listed as an inactive app: the search rail shows only Ended today, as before.

Weak

  • settings-your-apps: the word "Keep alive" repeats on every row beside its switch. It names each switch, but a column heading would be quieter.
  • home-kept: the closed tile shows no runway, though the session tiles do. Its account is known when its sessions ran on one.

99-new-session

99-new-session: New session, the usual made and said (0.4, D-62, AJ-E3207)

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written at 0.4 from its captures.

0.4

The principles audit (docs/design/PRINCIPLES.md, worked example): New session opened with "How it runs" and "Start from a saved handover…" on top, six folder cards with none chosen from the top bar, a "Ranked for you" list and a "Which account?" card that said the same pick twice, about 14 equal boxes before Customise. Now it opens on one stated choice, one fold and one button. Seven screens, both themes, opened from the top bar's New session on a Mac with ten projects and four Apps on three Accounts (Work, Claude Pro, Codex Plus; one App not signed in).

**usual** (N1). One tinted block, the only one: "Start in **web-shop** on **Claude Code · Work Max** — the last you used here". Under it two quiet lines, each with its link at the right: the folder ("~/Dev/web-shop · worked here 12 min ago", Change folder) and the App's Account ("Account **Work** · 58% left in the 5-hour window", Change app). Room is said once, for the Account. Then one line, "More options · Main · opus · runs as its launch settings say". The footer, on its own surface: Cancel and the one primary, "Start in web-shop ⌘↩", which has focus, so Return or ⌘↩ starts it. Three areas, one accent button.

**usual-1024.** The same at 1024: the sheet keeps its width, nothing wraps.

**change-folder** (N2). Change folder opens the shared picker on its search field ("Search projects…", ten projects): "Choose another folder…" first, then Recent (five, newest first, with path and when), Pinned (infra), then All projects. Typing "pay" and Return picks payments-api, and the sentence becomes "Start in payments-api on Codex · ~/.codex — the last you used here": the App follows the folder.

**change-app** (N2). "Which app": the four Apps by room, each with its Account and room ("Claude Pro · 82% left…"), the one in use ticked, the one not signed in last with "Not signed in". Four is under the picker's grouping threshold, so it's one list; with six or more it opens on Suggested and "Used in web-shop".

**more-options** (N3). Open, under a hairline indent: Launch settings (Work Max has two: Main, Quick fixes), How it runs, Start from a saved handover…, Expert setup… The fold's label still says the values, so a closed fold hides nothing.

**first-time** (N4). No projects, no sessions: "Start a session on **Claude Code · Personal** — the most room left"; the folder line says "None yet: it starts in a temporary folder" (what the backend does without one), and the button reads "Start on Claude Code · Personal".

**out-of-room** (N5). Work is at 0%: the sentence still names Work Max with its why, the room words go, and one warning line below a hairline says "Work is out of room until 4:20 PM: a session there would stop at its limit at once. Use Claude Code · Personal (82% left)". The button still starts on Work Max: nothing switches by itself (D-31).

Fixed while critiquing

  • The search field sat inside the picker's menu (axe: aria-required-children, serious); the shared RowMenu now puts a field above its menu, outside it, so every Picker with search passes.
  • Change folder and Change app showed the chosen value's icon beside the link (a folder beside "Change folder" under the folder's own icon); a Picker with its own words shows none.
  • "Work" alone on the second line didn't say what it was; it reads "Account Work".
  • The out-of-room line said "on this account"; it names the Account.
  • At a narrow window the folder's when-words ran under Change folder (seen in the flows gallery at 390 px); each line is one line that cuts at its end.
  • The Change app list said "Not measured yet" where the stated line says nothing for unknown room; both are silent now.
  • "Start from a saved handover…" kept its old bottom margin inside the fold, a gap above Expert setup.

Weak

  • The folder picker is tall (60 lines drawn) and opens upward over the sheet's title at 800 px; paging All projects, and Recent and Pinned not repeated in All, would shorten it.
  • Apps show the terminal glyph, not their tool's own icon.
  • The picker has no "Always use for web-shop" (the project's default) yet; it's set on the project page.
  • A fresh room reading never moves the pick (it's held once said), but nothing says "Personal now has more room · Use it" either.
  • Paired Macs and a started handover aren't in this gallery (open-pack and the remote sheet have their own).

99-palette: ⌘K — search, act or go

Design critique (D-57) against the reference screens: the row menus (77-menus), the rail's search and Settings' search. New family (AJ-E2901).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridOne dialog, field on top, groups under their labels, the footer in two lines (keys, then See all results and the go-to key); the preview takes a column on the right.
HierarchyGroup labels small and muted; each row's name, then where in muted words, then what choosing it does at the end.
SpacingThe menu rhythm; the dialog widens from 640 to 920 px only while the preview shows.
IconsEach row its own mark: the app's icon, the project's mark, the command's icon (the same as elsewhere).
Shared controlsThe shared Button in the preview and the footer; the combobox and listbox pattern with groups.
Disabled reasonsA selected item's greyed action keeps its reason (from the registry).
Plain words"Processes — 7 left running", "Notifications — 1 unread", "No matches for “qqqzz”.", "AI Juggler's service isn't answering — showing what was open last."
Type sizes13 px rows, 12 px labels and footer.
Both themesChecked; the matched letters are bold (not only coloured) in both.
1280×800, 1024×700 and 1440×900All three; the preview fits beside the list at 1024, and at 1440 the dialog keeps its width rather than stretching.

Good

  • Before typing it is useful: what you opened last, then the four things you do most, then what's open.
  • The matched letters are bold ("pay" in payments-api), and a row named by what's typed starts active, so ↩ opens it.
  • No matches leaves Search History for the words active, one ↩ away.

Weak

  • A loose letter match ("pay" in Privacy) still shows in Actions above the project it isn't; it is no longer the active row, but it reads as noise.
  • The footer's go-to key button reads long ("Choose a key to open this from any app…") beside See all results.

Fixed

  • First render (seen by eye): typing "pay" left "Go to Privacy" active, so ↩ went to Privacy, not payments-api. The first row whose name starts with what's typed now starts active (vitest: "a row named by what's typed starts active…").
  • First render: the footer's key hints wrapped mid-phrase on one line with the buttons; the hints have their own line now.

99-related-list

99-related-list: the one related list and the one session row (0.4, E31, AJ-E3103)

Design critique (D-57, AJ-E2814). First critique of this family, written from its captures (ui/tests/visual/related-list-gallery.spec.ts).

0.4

The fixture is one CLI App, Claude Code · Work Max, with eleven sessions: one that asks, two working, one idle, four that ended this week and three older ones. A second App, Side project, has none. Each screen is opened from the rail's App header, as a user would open it.

**app-sessions-long (1280 and 1024, both themes).** The Sessions tab reads "Sessions 11", and the tab's count is at full strength now (see Fixed). A search field fills the row, with two chips at its end: "State: All" and "Project: All". There is no App chip (this is the App's own page) and no Account chip (the App's folder is on one Account). The line "11 sessions · 4 open" heads one card with five section headings in order: Needs you, Working, Open, Last 7 days, Earlier.

Each row has the same anatomy:

  • the Claude Code icon, with the state's dot at its corner;
  • the title on line 1;
  • on line 2, the project's mark and name, then when;
  • the state word in its colour at the end of line 2: "Asks you" in amber, "Working" in the live colour, "Idle" and "Ended" muted.

This page is the App's, so the rows leave out the App's name. At 1024 the rail folds to icons and the rows keep both lines; nothing is cut except long titles.

**filter-menu (both themes).** The Project chip's menu is the shared Picker. It lists All ✓, then "ai-juggler · 5", "No project · 3" and "payments-api · 3": every value the list holds, each with how many rows it shows, the most rows first.

**filtered (both themes).** "State: Open now" and "Project: ai-juggler · 2" are lit, with "Clear filters" beside them. The heading reads "2 sessions · 2 open" over the two working rows. There are no section headings, because only one section remains.

**no-match (both themes).** A search for "kubernetes" leaves one card: "Nothing here matches “kubernetes”." and a "Clear the search" link. The Project chip goes away, since there's nothing left to narrow.

**app-empty (both themes).** Claude Code · Side project has no session yet. One card says "No sessions in Claude Code · Side project yet", then "Sessions you start on this folder, here or in a terminal, show up here, what needs you first.", then one primary button, New session.

**home-recent (both themes).** Home's "Pick up where you left off" draws its rows with the same row as the list:

  • the icon with a grey dot;
  • the title;
  • "payments-api · 5 h ago" on line 2, with "Ended" at its end;
  • "Mark reviewed" at the end of the title line.

In this narrow column the App's words fold away (container query); the icon still says which app it was.

Fixed while critiquing

  • Axe found that the chosen tab's count failed contrast: "11" at 70 % on the accent tint. The chosen tab's count is at full strength now; other tabs keep theirs dimmer than their name (pages/PageShell.tsx). This changes, by a few pixels, every page capture whose chosen tab has a count.
  • In the rail, a row with no project read "1 …" for "1 h ago": the time was capped against a project that wasn't there. It reads "1 h ago" now (row/RowParts.tsx).
  • Home's rows had "Mark reviewed" in a third column, which cut the project to "pa…". It now ends the title line, so the place line has the row's width.
  • "Mark reviewed" was 20 px tall (axe target-size). It is 24 px now.
  • The project filter's "No project" shared All's value: React warned about duplicate keys, and choosing it showed everything. It has a value of its own now.
  • Outside the gallery, the project page's Sessions tab counted only AI Juggler's open sessions in the design preview ("Sessions 3" over a list of 27). It counts what its list shows now (27).

Weak

  • At 1280 a row's state word sits about 850 px from its title, at the end of line 2. Read down, the states form a column; read across, the eye travels far. Columns for project and App at wide widths would fill the gap, but would give the row a second anatomy. Left for the maintainer's review.
  • A search that leaves fewer than two values takes that filter's chip away (no-match), so chips appear and disappear as you type.
  • On Home every row says "Ended". It is true, and it is the row's anatomy, but in a list that is all ended it repeats.
  • The fixture's projects have no marks, so every row shows the neutral folder tile. Real projects show their own marks.
  • "↑ 3 new" and "Show in order" are not captured: the design preview has no backend to say sessions changed (not-captured.md).

An empty list's step is a secondary button now: a CLI App with no sessions had two filled buttons (the header's New session here and the card's New session); the card also says "Sessions you start in this App", not "on this folder" (9d96ccd06).

0.4.1 re-score (2026-11-07, AJ-E3305)

The whole family was retaken on release/0.4.1 for the 14-line re-score (AJ-E3305, docs/qa/0.4-walks/design.md) and opened on contact sheets, both themes, 1280 and 1024. Shared with every screen since the last shoot: the header's New session is tinted (one filled button per view), the Floor button is a split control with its own menu, This Mac's line is its dot below 1120 px, and the rail's App headers put the profile first.

Unchanged in design: Relaunch then Resume, each with its line under it, "Ended 2:03 AM" once, This Mac on the facts line (a session's door to This Mac since 9b6b9c96b).

99-settings-keyboard

<!-- captures: pending -->

99-settings-keyboard: Settings › Keyboard

Design critique (D-57) against the reference screens: Settings' other panes (65-settings-sections), the ? sheet and Desktop apps' go-to key. New family (AJ-E2905).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridOne card per area; each row's name on the left, its keys in one column, its actions in a fixed-width slot on the right, so every key lines up whether a row has Change… or not.
HierarchyPane title, then the changed bar (only when something is changed), then the area titles over their cards.
SpacingThe settings cards' row rhythm; a fixed row's reason sits on a second line under its name.
IconsNone added: keys are keycaps, as on the ? sheet.
Shared controlsThe shared Button for Change…, Reset and Reset all; the go-to row is Desktop apps' own control (one capture component for both).
Disabled reasonsFixed shortcuts say why in words ("Esc closes what's open everywhere.", "⇧F10 is the system's key for a menu.").
Plain words"You changed 1 shortcut.", "⌘N is New session. Change that one first.", "Press the new keys, or Esc to cancel."
Type sizesRow names 14 px; keys and reasons 12 px.
Both themesDark and light checked; the refusal reads in amber in both.
1280×800 and 1024×700Both; the actions slot keeps its width at 1024.

Good

  • A changed key shows "changed" beside its keycap and a Reset beside Change…, and the bar at the top counts the changes, so a rebinding is never hidden (the task's handover).
  • A refused key says whose it is and what to do, under the row, without leaving capture.

Weak

  • The go-to row's "None" sits in the actions slot rather than the keys column, so its value is a column to the right of the others' keys.
  • Capturing has no visible timer or pulse beyond the accent border; the line under the row carries the instruction.

Fixed

  • First render (seen by eye): the capture button's "Press the keys… (Esc cancels)" wrapped onto two lines inside the actions slot. In Keyboard it now says "Press keys…" and the row's own line says "Press the new keys, or Esc to cancel."; Desktop apps keeps the full words.
  • Axe (label-content-name-mismatch): the capture's spoken name didn't contain its visible words. The visible words are now its name, with the shortcut added for screen readers only.

0.4.1 re-score (2026-11-07, AJ-E3305)

The whole family was retaken on release/0.4.1 for the 14-line re-score (AJ-E3305, docs/qa/0.4-walks/design.md) and opened on contact sheets, both themes, 1280 and 1024. Shared with every screen since the last shoot: the header's New session is tinted (one filled button per view), the Floor button is a split control with its own menu, This Mac's line is its dot below 1120 px, and the rail's App headers put the profile first.

Unchanged in design: the keycap is the button, "Press keys…" while capturing, "changed" with Reset on a changed row.

99-settings-search

<!-- captures: pending -->

99-settings-search: Settings' search

Design critique (D-57) against the rail's search field and Settings' sidebar (65-settings-sections). New family (AJ-E2905).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridThe field heads the sidebar at the panes' width; results open under it, on the sidebar's column.
HierarchyEach result: its pane small and muted over the setting's name.
SpacingResults on the menu rhythm; the panes below keep their places, dimmed.
IconsThe rail search field's ⌕, so both searches look alike.
Shared controlsThe rail's search field styling; the listbox is the combobox pattern (↑↓ ↩).
Disabled reasonsn/a: panes without a match dim but stay clickable.
Plain words"No setting matches “nothing like this”."
Type sizes13 px field and names, 12 px pane labels.
Both themesChecked; the dimmed panes are still legible in light.
1280×800 and 1024×700Both; at 1024 the "no match" line wraps inside the sidebar without cutting.

Good

  • Picking a result opens the pane at the section and moves focus to its title (opened captures show the focus ring on "Alerts and the digest").
  • The index finds settings by other words ("mute" finds Notifications › Muted).

Weak

  • One result shows as a single short card; with many results the list has no count.
  • The focused section title's ring spans the whole row width, heavier than the other focus rings.

Fixed

  • Axe (aria-required-children, listitem): "No setting matches" was a plain item inside the listbox. It is now a status line under the field, and the combobox points at a list only while there are results.

99-usage-cost

<!-- captures: pending -->

99-usage-cost: cost you can act on

Design critique (D-57) against Usage's tiles and Top sessions (94-usage) and the shared sheet. New family (AJ-E2902).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridTwo tiles side by side on the dashboard's grid, then Cost by account as a full-width table; the review sheet stacks the same parts.
HierarchyTile label, ~figure, one line of what it counts; the table's header row over its rows; the price list's date in the section's sub line.
SpacingThe dashboard's card rhythm; table rows at the Top sessions height.
IconsThe money and tokens icons Usage's tiles use.
Shared controlsStatTile, Section, the shared sheet and its paged list; Top sessions' row for every session.
Disabled reasonsn/a. Unknown is words: "No finished tasks in this range", "Nothing went on crashed or stuck sessions", "No list price".
Plain words"On 13 sessions: 10 crashed, 3 probably stuck — see them", "API-equivalent estimate — your subscription price doesn't change per token."
Type sizesTiles' figures at the tiles' size; table 14 px, sub lines 12 px.
Both themesChecked; "Crashed" and "Probably stuck…" read in amber in both.
1280×800 and 1024×700Both; the table keeps its five columns at 1024.

Good

  • Every figure says what it's of ("65 finished · 51 other apps' not counted"), so a reader can't take an average of AI Juggler's sessions for all sessions.
  • The wasted sessions are a click away and each says why it's there.
  • The review puts last week beside the week before with the change, and exports just that week.

Weak

  • "Price" says "list price" on every row in the preview; the column earns its place only when a model lacks a price.
  • The review's tiles repeat the dashboard's look closely; a reader may not notice they're a different week until the subtitle.

Fixed

  • First render (seen by eye): the review opened scrolled down at 1024, focus on the table's first account button; it now opens at its top.
  • Axe (scrollable-region-focusable): the wasted sheet's list scrolled but couldn't be reached by keyboard when its rows held no button. A paged list that scrolls on its own is now a focusable, named region (every sheet using it).

0.4.1 re-score (2026-11-07, AJ-E3305)

The whole family was retaken on release/0.4.1 for the 14-line re-score (AJ-E3305, docs/qa/0.4-walks/design.md) and opened on contact sheets, both themes, 1280 and 1024. Shared with every screen since the last shoot: the header's New session is tinted (one filled button per view), the Floor button is a split control with its own menu, This Mac's line is its dot below 1120 px, and the rail's App headers put the profile first.

"3.2M tokens a task · 65 finished · 51 other apps' not counted" reads "· not counting 51 sessions other apps ran" (9d96ccd06).

99-when-to-tell: Notifications › When to tell me

Design critique (D-57) against Settings › Notifications (84-notifications). New family (AJ-E2904).

Checklist

CheckFinding
GridUnder the kinds of notice: the wait, the two quota levels and Send test, each a line with its picker inline.
HierarchyThe sub-heading, each line a sentence with its choice in it.
SpacingThe notifications card's rhythm.
IconsThe pickers' chevrons.
Shared controlsThe shared Picker and Button.
Disabled reasonsn/a.
Plain words"Tell me when a session waits longer than 5 minutes", "Needs you and the bell show a wait at once either way."
Type sizes13 px lines.
Both themesChecked dark and light.
1280×800 and 1024×700Both.

Good

  • Each setting reads as the sentence it changes.
  • It says the in-app signals don't wait.

Weak

  • Send test sits alone under the quota line; its result is said only after a click.

Fixed

  • Nothing on first render.
